aṅṅaṉam vāḷic cuṟṟiyāṭum pōtu oru maṟavaṉ vāḷ muṉiyil vīḻntu uyirviṭuvāṉ. itu maṟaccāvu eṉappaṭum. oruvaṉ vāḷ koṇṭu uṭampil puṇṇuṇṭākkik koḷvāṉ. itu maṟakkuṟippu eṉappaṭum. iviyum koṟṟavi niliyē yākum. vaḻivaḻi vanta maṟakkuṭiyiṉarallarō ammaṟavar! iṅṅaṉam palar ceyvareṉka.
3. vañci (9-7)
taṉṉāṭṭukkum aṭutta nāṭṭukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa potu nilattit taṉatākka eṇṇi, aṭutta nāṭṭaracaṉi vellutaṟkup paṭiyeṭuttuc cellutal vañcittiṇi eṉappaṭum. oruvaṉ maṇṇāciyāl paṭiyeṭuttuc celliṉ, avaṉum ammaṇṇi yiḻakkāmaṟ kāttaṟku etirē varutaliṉ - etirttaliṉ - avviruvarum maṇṇāciyāl mēṟcellum vañci vēntar eṉappaṭuvar. vañci - paṭiyeṭuttuc cellutal. naci - viruppam.
vañcittiṇi veṭcittiṇit toṭarpēyātalāl, veṭcip potuvākiya avviṭi nilattit taṉatākkik koḷḷap paṭiyeṭuttuc celvāṉeṉka. iṅṅaṉam aṉṟip paṭiyeṭut taluṅ koḷka.
vañcimaṟavar ceyal (puṟa - 8)
nālvakip paṭiyum pakivar añcumpaṭi āravārittuk koṇṭu cellum. aṅṅaṉam cellumpōtu, pakivar nāṭṭil eṅkum tīpparavumpaṭi maṟavarkaḷ neruppi eṭuttup pakivar ūrkaḷic cuṭuvar. itu eḷitil pakivari vellutaṟkuk kiyāḷum ceyal eṉṟālum koṭumiyēyākum. iṉṟum ivvūr cuṭuṅ koṭumi pōr muṟiyiliruntu oḻintapāṭilli.
pakivēntar iruvarkkum palar paṭittuṇiyāvar. ataṉāl, iruvarum perumi yeytuvar. vañcivēntar iruvarum tam paṭiyāḷarkaṭkup paṭikkalaṅkaḷ koṭuttup perumip paṭuttuvar. ēṉāti, kāviti mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum; nāṭu, ūr mutaliyaṉavuṅ koṭuttuc cilarip perumip paṭuttip paṭit talivarākkuvar. appaṭit talivarkaḷ paki vēntari oru poruḷāka matiyātu paṭiyic celuttuvar. aṉṉār paṭi celuttum pērāṇmiyik kaṇṭōr pukaḻntu kūṟuvar.
virintu varum āṟṟu veḷḷattik kallaṇi tāṅkuvatu pōla, vīraṉoruvaṉ taṉ mēl muṉintu varum perumpaṭi yiṉit tāṉoruvaṉē taṭuttu niṉṟu perumi yeytuvāṉ. ivaṉ muṉṉarp paṭṭaṅkoṭuttup paṭit talivarākkap paṭṭavaruḷ oruvaṉāvāṉ.
aracaṉ pōr ceyyat tuṇinta piṉ, paṭiyāḷarkku oru peruviruntu ceyvāṉ. avviruntil tāṉum uṭaṉiruntuṇṭu paṭiñari ūkkuvippāṉ. pōril veṉṟa vēntaṉ tōṟṟa vēntaṉukkē avaṉ nāṭṭik koṭuttut tiṟi tarumpaṭi ceytu mīḷvāṉ. ataṉāl, paḻantamiḻvēntarkaḷ maṇṇāci yoṉṟē kāraṇamākap pōrpuriyavilliyeṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa taṉṟō? veṉṟa vēntaṉ viḷakkattiyum, tōṟṟut tiṟi taru niliyi yaṭintōṉ kuṟiyiyum, kaṇṭōr kūṟuvar. ikkaṇṭōrē varalāṟṟuc cōliyi iṭiyaṟātu vaḷarttu varuvōrāvar.
veṉṟa vēntaṉatu veṟṟic ciṟappiṉāl tōṟṟōṉ nāṭu aḻitaṟ kiraṅki, tōṟṟa vēntaṉ ciṟappiṉmiyi veṉṟa vēntaṉ nāṭṭu makaḷir pāṭuvar. itu vaḷḷippāṭṭu allatu koṟṟa vaḷḷi eṉappaṭum. vaḷḷi uraṟpāṭṭu. atāvatu, veṉṟa vēntaṉ nāṭṭu makaḷir nel mutaliyaṉa kuṟṟum pōtu, tam maracaṉatu veṟṟiyic ciṟappittup pāṭik koṇṭu kuṟṟuvareṉka. nammaracaṉōṭu pakittataṉāl avaṉ nāṭu innilimiyāṉatu eṉṉum karuttamiyap pāṭuvar eṉka. aḻital - keṭutal.
iḵtu akkālap peṇmakkaḷiṉ araciyalaṟiviyum, maṟak kuṟippiyum, nāṭṭup paṟṟiyum pulappaṭuttum. iṉṟu peṇ maṇikaḷ nāṭṭu naṭappuk kēṟṟavāṟu aḻukiṟa pāṭṭup (oppāri) puṉintu koḷvatu pōlap puṉintu pāṭuvatākum ivvaḷḷip pāṭṭu.
iṅṅaṉam pakivar nāṭaḻitaṟ kiraṅkip pulavar pāṭutalum uṇṭu. atu pāṭāṇ koṟṟavaḷḷi yākum (puṟa - 34)
pōr muṭintapiṉ puṇpaṭṭa vīrarkaḷi aracaṉ taṉit taṉiyē taḻuvi makiḻvippāṉ. itu paḻantamiḻaracarkaḷ makkaḷiṭam vittiruntaaṉpiyum, ceynnaṉṟiyaṟitaliyum, kaṭappāṭṭu ṇarcciyiyum kāṭṭuvatākum.
4. uḻiñi (puṟa - 10)
oruvaṉ oru nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟāl, etirniṟkalāṟṟāta annāṭṭu vēntaṉ kōṭṭik katavukaḷi yaṭittu, ataṉuḷpātukāppāka iruntu koḷvāṉ. paṭi eṭuttuc ceṉṟa vēntaṉ ammatili - kōṭṭiyi - muṟṟukiyiṭuvāṉ. matili vaḷittalum, kāttalum, uḻiñi eṉappaṭum. muṟṟuki yiṭuvōṉ - puṟattuḻiñi yōṉ, puṟattōṉ eṉavum, matilaṭit tiruppōṉ - akattuḻiñiyōṉ, akattōṉ eṉavum peyar peṟuvar. matil, kōṭṭi, araṇ, eyil - oru poruṭ coṟkaḷ.
1. kōṭṭi
paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ kaṟṟaccu - ciṟpam, ōviyak kalikaḷiṟ pōlavē kaṭṭiṭak kaliyilum oppuyarvaṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉar eṉpataṟku, paḻantamiḻ vēntarkaḷiṉ kōṭṭikaḷē cāṉṟākum. paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭuk kōṭṭiyiṉ amippu aṟintu viyakkattakka toṉṟākum.
akkōṭṭi puṟamatil, iṭimatil, akamatil eṉṉum mummatilkaḷi yuṭiyatāyirukkum. ovvoru matiliṉ puṟattēyum āḻntakaṉṟu akaḻum, puṟamatiliṉ akaḻukkup puṟattē periya toru kāvaṟkāṭum poruntiyirukkum. itu muḻumutal araṇam (puṟa - 10) eṉappaṭum.
kāvaṟkāṭṭil muḷḷuḷḷa maram ceṭi koṭikaḷ aṭarn tirukkum, virintōṭum pakivar kālkaḷiṟ ṟippataṟkāka neruñcimuṭpōṉṟa iruppumuṭkaḷ akkāṭeṅkum parappap paṭṭirukkum. ōṭuvōr kālil māṭṭiyiḻuttut taḷḷuvataṟkākat tōṭṭi eṉṉum irumpuk kokkikaḷ akkāvaṟ kāṭeṅkum muḷiyaṭittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. kāṭṭic cuṟṟipperum paṭi kāvalirukkum. yāṉik kāvalum uṇṭu.
ikkāvaṟ kāṭṭukkum, kiṭaṅkukkum iṭiyē pakivar matili yaṇukāmal pārttukkoḷḷa, marañceṭi koṭikaḷ illāta marunilam eṉṉum veṭṭa veḷinilam irukkum.
matilkaḷiṉ puṟattēyuḷḷa kiṭaṅkukaḷil koṭiya periya mutalikaḷ niṟiya irukkum. kōṭṭikkuṭpuka nāṟpuṟamum vāyilkaḷ amintirukkum. akaḻiyit tāṇṭik kōṭṭikkuṭ celvataṟku vāyil vaḻiyil palaki pōṭappaṭṭirukkum. ippala kiyip pōkkuvaravu illātapōtu uḷḷiḻuttuk koḷvar.
cila kōṭṭikaḷ malikaṭkiṭiyiṉum kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. eṉavē, kōṭṭi - nīraraṉ, nilavaraṇ, kāṭṭaraṇ, maliyaraṇ eṉṉum nālvakiyaraṇkaḷāl cūḻappaṭṭirukku meṉpatu peṟappaṭum. itaṉi,
“maṇinīrum maṇṇum maliyumaṇiniḻaṟ
 kāṭum uṭiya taraṇ.”	(kuṟaḷ)
eṉṟār vaḷḷuvar.
kōṭṭi vāyilkaḷukkup periya irumpuk katavukaḷ pōṭappaṭṭirukkum. marakkatavukaḷum mikap periyatāka irukkum. kataviṉ nilavukāl mikka vali poruntiya pala maraṅkaḷiṇittuc ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. kataviṉ uṭpuṟattil, veḷiyiliruntu katavi eḷitil tiṟakka muṭiyātapaṭi, nilavukāliyaṭuttu irupuṟamum eḻu eṉṉum periya maraṅkaḷ nāṭṭappaṭṭu, avviru eḻuviṉukkum iṭiyē cīppu eṉṉum kuṟukku maram, eḻukkaḷil uḷḷa aiyavi eṉṉum tāṅku kaṭṭiyiṉ mēl pōṭappaṭṭirukkum. aiyavi - tulākkaṭṭi. itu aiyavittulām eṉavum peyar peṟum.
periya kalluralkaḷiyum, periya maraṅkaḷiyum tūkkik kaṭṭi, yāṉikaḷik koṇṭu aṭittālum akkatavi uṭikkavō, tiṟakkavō muṭiyātu. veḷiyiliruntu yāṉikaḷik koṇṭu taḷḷiṉālum tiṟakka muṭiyāmal uḷḷiruntu taḷḷip piṭippataṟku eppōtum valiporuntiya mallar palar aṅku kāvaliruppar. uḷḷiruntu yāṉikaḷum taḷḷip piṭikkum.
nilavukaḷuk karukē uṭpuṟam matiṟ cuvaril uḷḷa aṟikkuḷ pōrk karuvikaḷuṭaṉ maṟavarkaḷ taṅkiyiruntu vāyil vaḻiyāka uṭpuka muyalum pakivarit tākkuvar. ivvaṟi iru nilavup pakkamum irukkum. periya kōyilkaḷiṉ mutaṟ kōpura vāyilil ittakiya aṟikaḷ iruttaliyaṟika.
matil mukaṭṭil, matilic cuṟṟilum pataṇam eṉṉum mēṭi amintirukkum. immēṭikku veḷiyē vali poruntiya cuṟṟuc cuvar amintiruppatiṉāl immēṭi matiluḷmēṭi eṉappaṭum. ammēṭiyil, pakivar matiṉ mēl ēṟāmal taṭukkavum, tākkavum palavakip pōrk karuvikaḷum poṟikaḷum vikkappaṭṭirukkum. karuvi - nām piṭittu iyakkuvatu. poṟi - tāṉākavē iyaṅkuvatu. ammatiluḷmēṭiyākiya pataṇattiṉ kīḻ uḷḷēyiruntu pakivar mēl ampeyyum tuḷikaḷ poruntiya ēppuḻi ñāyil eṉṉum aṟiyoṉṟu matilic cuṟṟilum amintirukkum. avvaṟikkuḷ maṟavarkaḷ iruntu kāval kāttuvaruvar. ē-ampu. puḻi - tuḷi. ñāyil-aṟi. itu ēvaṟi eṉappaṭum. pataṇattilum ēvaṟiyilumuḷḷa maṟavarkaḷ. pakivarkaḷ matil mēl ēṟa muṭiyāta paṭi tākkuvar. immatil muṭiyuṟuppukkaḷ iraṇṭum - pataṇamum, ēvaṟiyum - mūṉṟu matilkaḷilum amintirukkum.
matiliṉ mēl nāṉku mūliyilum, tūṇpōl mikavum uyarntakaṉṟa kaṭṭiṭam amintirukkum. atu kottaḷam eṉappaṭum. akkottaḷattiṉ mēl iravu pakal ennēramum maṟavarkaḷiruntu, neṭuntolivil varum paki variyaṟintu colvar. itu, ‘kōṭṭi kottaḷam’ eṉakkōṭṭiyōṭu cērttu vaḻaṅkappaṭum kōṭṭimatil.
2. karuvikaḷum poṟikaḷum
matiluḷ mēṭiyil uḷḷa karuvikaḷum poṟikaḷumāvaṉa :
“miḷiyum kiṭaṅkum vaḷiviṟ poṟiyum
 karuviral ūkamum kallumiḻ kavaṇum
 parivuṟu venneyum pākaṭu kuḻiciyum
 kāypoṉ ṉuliyum kalliṭu kūṭiyum
 tūṇṭilum toṭakkum āṇṭali yaṭuppum
 kaviyum kaḻuvum putiyum puḻiyum
 aiyavit tulāmum kipeya ruciyum
 ceṉṟeṟi ciralum paṉṟiyum paṇiyum
 eḻuvum cīppum muḻuviṟaṟ kaṇiyamum
 kōlum kuntamum vēlum piṟavum
 ñāyilum ciṟantu nāṭkoṭi nuṭaṅkum
 vāyil”
(cilappatikāram - 15 207 - 18)
miḷi - kāvaṟkāṭu. kiṭaṅku - akaḻ. 1. vaḷi vil poṟi - vaḷintu tāṉē ampeyyum viṟpoṟi. 2. karuviral ūkam - kariya viralkaḷiyuṭiya kuraṅkupōṉṟa kaṭikkum poṟi. itu kuraṅkup poṟi. 3.  kal umiḻ kavaṇ - kalleṟiyum kavaṇ. 4. parivuṟu venney - matilmī tēṟa muyalum pakivarmītu kāycci ūṟṟum ney. 5. pāku aṭu kuḻici - eṇṇey kāyccavum, cempurukkavum karitta cāṇaṅ kāyccavum, ati ūṟṟi vikkavum āṉa miṭākkaḷ. kuḻuci - itu kūci eṉa marīi vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. 6. kāy poṉ uli - irumpuk kampikaḷik kāyccum uli. poṉ - irumpu. 7. kal iṭu kūṭi - kavaṇil vitteṟiyum kaṟkaḷ pōṭṭuvikkum kūṭi. 8. tūṇṭil - matil mītu ēṟuvōri māṭṭi iḻukkum tūṇṭil. 9. toṭakku - kaḻuttil māṭṭi muṟukkum caṅkili. 10. āṇṭali aṭuppu - matilmēlēṟuvōr taliyik kotti mūḷiyik kaṭikkum āṇṭalip poṟi. āṇṭali - āṇ makaṉ talipōṉṟa oruvakippaṟavi; itu āṇṭalippuḷ eṉappaṭum. aṭuppukkal pōl mūṉṟu oṉṟāka iruppatāl, āṇṭali aṭuppu eṉappaṭṭatu. 11. kavi - matilmēlēṟuvōr kaḻuttil koṭuttuk kīḻē taḷḷutaṟkāṉa irumpuk kavi. 12. kaḻu - iruppulakki. 13. puti - ampukkaṭṭu. 14. puḻi - vennīri ūṟṟi matil mēlēṟuvōr mukattil aṭikkum kuḻāy; tī vīcuṅ kuḻāyumām. 15. kipeyar ūci - matil muṭiyilēṟum pakivar kiyik kuttum ūci. 16. ceṉṟeṟi ciral - matilmē lēṟuvōrik kottum mīṉkottip paṟavi pōṉṟa poṟi. 17. paṉṟip poṟi. 18. paṇi - mūṅkil pōṉṟa irumpuk kampikaḷ. 19. kaṇiyam - vaḷitaṭi. 20 - 22 īṭṭi, vāḷ, vēl.
piṟa - 23. arinūṟ poṟi - matilmēlēṟum pakivar uṭali aṟukkum nūlpōṉṟa poṟi. 24. nūṟṟuvarik kolli - orē eṭuppil nūṟu pērik kollum poṟi. 25. taḷḷi veṭṭi. 26. kaḷiṟṟup poṟi. 27. pulippoṟi. 28. takarppoṟi. 29. kaḻukup poṟi. 30. viḻuṅku pāmpu. 31. kuṭappāmpu. 32. vaṇṭippoṟi mutaliyaṉa.
ivaṟṟuḷ : 1, 2, 10, 15, 16, 17, 23, 24, 26, 27, 28, 29, 30, 31, 32 ippatiṉintum poṟikaḷ. maṟṟavi karuvikaḷ. poṟi - miṉcāram pōṉṟa yātō ōr āṟṟaliṉāl tāṉē iyaṅkuvatu.
kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiya kumarik kaṇṭattil vāḻnta paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ, taṇṇīrit taḷḷum oruvakik kallik kappaliṉ piṉpuṟam amittu, ataṉāl kappali iyakki vantaṉarām. neṭun tolivil oḷivīcum oru poruḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉarām. attakiya ētāvatoṉṟu appoṟikaḷi iyakkiyirukkalām.
poṟikaḷi iyakkum vici matiṟ cuvaril poruttap paṭṭu, matilmēlēṟuvōr kikālkaḷ avviciyiṟ paṭṭatum ap poṟikaḷ tāmē ceṉṟu aṉṉārit tākkiṉa pōlum. atāvatu, pakivar matiṉmēlēṟat toṭaṅkiṉatum appoṟikaḷ ceṉṟu avarit tākkumpaṭi avi amikkap paṭṭirukkum. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ aracakumarar ākiyāl, tamatu araṇmaṉi matilil irunta appoṟikaḷi, avaṟṟiṉ toḻiṟṟiṟatti aṟintavarēyāvar.
cīvakaṉ tāyākiya vicayi eṉpāḷ, oru mayiṟpoṟi mītēṟi, amaraṇmaṉikkuḷḷiruntu paṟantu veḷiyē ceṉṟatākac cintāmaṇi kūṟukiṟatu (238). avantināṭṭaracaṉāṉa piraccōtaṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, utayaṇaṉip piṭittuvarac ceytaṉuppiya yāṉip poṟi mukil pōl piḷiṟikkoṇṭu kāṭṭiṉ kaṇ ulāviyatu eṉap peruṅkati kūṟukiṟatu. atē peruṅkatiyil, utayaṇaṉ amiccaṉāṉa yūki eṉpāṉ, yavaṉattaccarāl ceyta pūṇiyiṉṟip poṟiyiṉ iyaṅkum oru vāṉūrtiyi ūrntu koṇṭu vāṉvaḻiyākac cayantinakar ceṉṟaṉaṉ eṉkiṉṟatu. tūṅkeyil eṟinta toṭittōṭ cempiyaṉ eṉṉum cōḻa maṉṉaṉ vāṉūrtiyoṉṟi maṇṇil vīḻttiṉāṉ eṉkiṉṟatu maṇimēkali. civaṉ muppurameritta katiyoṉṟuṇṭu.
ēḻāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ (1952il) mēṉāṭṭu aṟivar (viññāṉi) oruvar ōr āmip poṟiyik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷa tākac ceytit tāḷkaḷil veḷivantatu. atu miṉcāra mūḷiyuṭaṉ amintatu; tāṉākavē naṭattal, uṇavuṇṇal, avar coṉṉa paṭi kēṭṭal mutaliya ceyalkaḷ ceykiṉṟatām appoṟi, ittakiyatē piraccōtaṉaṉāl amikkappaṭṭa vēḻappoṟi.
 yūki avvūrtiyiluḷḷa ‘vittaka āṇi vēṇṭuvayiṉ muṟukki viṇṇakat tiḻintaṉaṉ’ (peruṅ 2: 193 - 4) eṉpatāl, iṉṟiya vāṉūrti pōlavē iyakkāṇiyāl poṟiyi iyakki avvūrtiyum ūrappaṭṭatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
ivaṟṟāl, iṉṟuḷḷa ūri (kār), vāṉūrti pōṉṟavi aṉṟum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatil ciṟitum aiyamilli.
iṉṟu vāṉoli mūlam ulaka nikaḻccikaḷik kēṭpatu pōlavē, aṉṟum vāṉoli eṉpataṉ mūlam veḷippēccik kēṭṭu vantaṉar. āṉāl, atu iṉṟupōlaṉṟi aṉṟu araciya lārkku maṭṭum terintatāka, uriyatāka iruntu vantatākat terikiṟatu.
eṉavē, immatiluḷmēṭiyil amikkappaṭṭirunta poṟikaḷum attakiyiṉavēyākum. etaṉāl iyaṅkiṉa eṉpatē tuṇintu kūṟa muṭiyavilli. itu ārāyccikkuriyatu. ikkōṭṭi yoṉṟē paḻantamiḻar nākarikattiṉ naṉimiku ciṟappik kāṭṭu taṟkup pōtumāṉatākum.
3. matilakam
ivvāṟu amintuḷḷa kōṭṭiyil, akamatiṟkuḷḷē yuḷḷa kōyilil aracakuṭumpattiṉar iruppar. kō + il - kōvil, kōyil. kō - aracaṉ, il - vīṭu. akamatiṟkum, iṭimatiṟkum, iṭippaṭṭatu ūr eṉappaṭum. ūril amiccar, paṭittalivar, vaṇikar, uḻuvit tuṇṇum vēḷāḷar mutaliya celvarkaḷiruppar. iṭimatiṟkum, puṟamatiṟkum iṭip paṭṭatu puṟañcēri eṉappaṭum. cēri - cērntu irukkum iṭam. puṟañcēriyil ellāk kuṭimakkaḷum iruppar. iṅkē mikunta parappuṭiya viḷi nilamum, vaṟṟā nīrkkiṇaṟukaḷum irukkum. kōṭṭikkuḷ iruppōrkkup pala āṇṭukaṭku vēṇṭiya uṇavup poruḷ cērttu vikkum pala periya kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ kōṭṭikkuḷ irukkum. eṉavē, pakivar palavāṇṭukaḷ matili muṟṟukiyiṭṭa pōtilum uḷḷiruppōrkku uṇavut taṭṭutal uṇṭākātu. cēlam māvaṭṭattu āṟṟūrk (āttūr) kōṭṭikkuḷ uḷḷa kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ iṉṟum appaḻaṅ kōṭṭikaḷukkuc cāṉṟāka niṉṟu nilavukiṉṟaṉa.
immūṉṟu matilkaḷilum pōr naṭakkum. akamatilik kaṭantu kōyil kikkoṇṭāltāṉ kōṭṭiyip piṭitta tākum. ‘kōṭṭi viṭṭuviṭṭāṉ’ eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyāl, paḻantamiḻk kōṭṭikaḷiṉ ciṟappu viḷaṅkum.
4. puṟattōṉum akattōṉum (puṟa - 12)
matil muṟṟum puṟattuḻiñiyōṉ ciṟappum, aṭit tirukkum akattuḻiñiyōṉ ciṟappum paḻantamiḻ vēntarkaḷiṉ maṟac ceyalukku viḷakkantaruvatu pōl amintuḷḷaṉa.
puṟattōṉ
tāṉ muṟṟiyuḷḷa matilik koḷvataṟku muṉṉarē am matiluk kuṭiyavaṉ nāṭṭi vēṇṭiyōrkkuk koṭukkum āṇmikkappāṟpaṭṭa maṟakkuṟippu aṟintu iṉpuṟaṟ pāla toṉṟākum. itu, ‘koḷḷār tēem kuṟitta koṟṟam’ eṉappaṭum. uṟutiyāka velvōm eṉṉum maṉaveḻucciyē itaṟkuk kāraṇa mākum. niṉittati muṭikka valla appuṟattōṉ ciṟappiṉi akattōṉ pāṟ ceṉṟa tūtuvar eṭutturittu, avaṉip paṇitalē naṉṟeṉak kūṟuvar. akattōṉ paṇiya maṟukkiṉ, paḻamiyākiya ikkōṭṭiyi iṉṟē aḻittu viṭukiṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi, ati aḻikkat toṭaṅkuvāṉ. aṅṅaṉam aḻikkat toṭaṅkiya avaṉ, pakivar vāḷum vēluṅ koṇṭu tākkiṉāl avaṟṟit taṭuppataṟkāka mikutiyāṉa kiṭukup paṭiyik koṇṭu celvāṉ. kiṭuku - kēṭayam. itu mutal matiṟpōr toṭaṅkum.
akattōṉ
akkōṭṭiyuḷ vaṟṟāta nīrkkiṇaṟukaḷum, kuṟiyāta neṟkaḷañciyaṅkaḷum pataṇattilum, uḷḷum tīrāta paṭikkalaṅ kaḷum uṇmiyi akattōṉ tūtar vantu puṟattōṉiṭam kūṟi, evvaḷavu kālam muṟṟiṉum payaṉilli, muṟṟuviṭṭuc celka veṉat terivippar. pāri paṟampi muṟṟukiyiṭṭa vēntari nōkkik kapilar kūṟiyatu (puṟanā - 109) itaṉpāṟpaṭum. atu kēṭṭum puṟattōṉ muṟṟuki viṭṭuc cellāṉeṉiṉ, akattōṉ puṟampōntu kaṭumpōr ceytu puṟattōṉik kalaṅkac ceyvāṉ. puṟattōr kōṭṭiyik kippaṟṟiṉum, orucila akattōr maṉaṅkalaṅkāmalum, paṇiyāmalum kaḷam vakuttup pōr ceyvar.
“uyar vakalan tiṇmi arumiyin nāṉkiṉ
 amivaraṇ eṉṟurikkum nūl.” (kuṟaḷ)
eṉṟa matililakkaṇappaṭi, taṉ matil ēṇikku eṭṭāta uyaramum, pakivarāl veḷiyiliruntu tuḷi ceyya muṭiyāta aṭiyakalamum, tammavar (akattōr) niṉṟu pōr ceytaṟkēṟṟa muṭiyakalamum, karuṅkallālum, vaṉcuṇṇattālum kaṭṭappaṭṭatāl eḷitil iṭikka muṭiyāta tiṇmiyum, poṟikaḷum, karuvikaḷum, maṟañ cāṉṟa maṟavarum uṭimiyāl, akattōṉ puṟattōṉi matiyāmal iruppāṉ.
5. matiṟpōr (puṟa - 13)
puṟattōṉ kōṭṭikkuṭ pukattoṭaṅkum nāḷil kuṭiyiyuyarttiyum, vāḷi nāṭṭiyum avaṉ maṟavarkaḷ toṭakka viḻāk koṇṭāṭuvar. ivi kuṭināṭkōḷ vāṇāṭkōḷ eṉappaṭum. vēli naṭutalum koḷka. itu iṉṟu koṇṭāṭum koṭiyēṟṟu viḻāp pōṉṟatu. kuṭi - kuṭikāttaṟkum, vāḷ - etiri kēṭṭiṟkum aṟikuṟiyākum. akattōṉum taṉakkōr vēntaṉ tuṇivantāl, veḷip pōntu pōr ceytaṟkāka nāṭkoḷvāṉ. nāṭkōṭal - pōrt toṭakkatti aṟivikkum aṟikuṟiyākum. maṟavarkku maṟañciṟattaṟkum innāṭkōṭal ētuvākum.
akaḻiṉ mītu - nīrmēl - vikkiṉṟa palakiyōṭu cērttuc ceyyappaṭṭa ēṇimēl niṉṟu akattōruṭaṉ puṟattōr pōr ceyvar. matilōrattil akaḻiyiruppatāl akaḻnīriṉ mēl mitakkum palakiyōṭu cērttē matil mēl cārttum ēṇi ceyyappaṭum. itu maṭiyami ēṇi eṉappaṭum. itu nam muṉṉi yōr viṉiceyal vakiyiṉ matinuṭpattik kāṭṭum. puṟattōr ēṇimēl ēṟum pōtutāṉ pataṇattiluḷḷa akattōr āṅ kuḷḷa karuvikaḷik koṇṭu matilmēlēṟaviṭāmal tākkuvar. āṅkuḷḷa poṟikaḷ ēṇimēlēṟuvōrit tākkum. ēvaṟiyiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa maṟavarkaḷ avar mītu ampumāri poḻivar. itu mikamikak koṭumiyāṉa pōr. mēlēṟēṟa veṭṭittaḷḷum maṟavar talikaḷ paṉaṅkāy pōl akaḻikkuḷ viḻum. akaḻikkuḷ vāyit tiṟantu koṇṭirukkum mutalikaḷ attalikaḷi appaṭiyē viḻuṅkiviṭum. niṉittālē neñcu tiṭukkiṭum pōrākum itu. appaṭiyiruntum puṟattōr matilmēlēṟip pataṇattuḷ kutittu viṭuvar. matil muṭiyilum pōr naṭakkum.
piṉṉar appuṟattuḻiñiyōr appuṟamatilik kik koṇṭu iṭimatili aṭiya muyalvar. akattōr viṭāmal etirttut tākkuvar. avvūrc ceruvil akattōr velvatum uṇṭu. ūrc ceruvi veṉṟu puṟattōr iṭimatili aṭivar. iruvaki maṟavarum avviṭimatiṟ kiṭaṅkiṉ irupuṟattilum niṉṟu nīrppācipōl nīṅkātu poruvar. itu pāci eṉappaṭum. muṉṉar naṭanta ūrcceru pācimaṟam eṉappaṭum. puṟattōr ivvakaḻppōril veṟṟi peṟiṉ, iṭimatiliṟ pōr naṭakkum. ivvāṟē akamatiliṉ kaṇṇum pōr naṭakkum.
puṟamatiṟ kiṭaṅku, puṟamatil, puṟamatiṟkum iṭimatiṟkum iṭiyiluḷḷa ūr, iṭimatiṟ kiṭaṅku, iṭimatil, akamatil ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil naṭanta pōrkaḷil veṉṟa vēntaṉ, tōṟṟa vēntaṉ muṭiyip puṉintu nīrāṭuvāṉ. itu maṇṇumaṅkalam eṉappaṭum. piṉṉar veṉṟa vāḷiṉi niṟutti nīrāṭṭuvar. itu vāḷmaṅkalam eṉappaṭum. piṉṉar avveṟṟikkuk kāraṇamāṉa maṟavarkkuc ciṟappuc ceyvāṉ.
5. tumpi (puṟa - 15)
taṉatu valiyiṉi ulakam pukaḻtalē tāṉ peṟuporuḷāka eṇṇi vanta vēntaṉi, māṟṟu vēntaṉum avvāṟē eṇṇi etirttup poruvatu tumpit tiṇi eṉappaṭum. tumpi - pōr.
cōmpaṉiyum, malaṭaṉiyum, mayir aviḻntōṉiyum, piṉvāṅkiṉōṉiyum, paṭiyiḻantōṉiyum, otta paṭi yeṭātōṉiyum kollār. iḵtu aṟappōr eṉappaṭum.
1. pōr (puṟa - 16)
iru peru vēntarum kaḷam vakuttaṉar. pōrppaṟi muḻaṅkiṉa. pāṭiyiliruntu maṟavarkaḷ pōrkkōlam pūṇṭaṉar. pōr toṭaṅkiṟṟu. oru peru vīraṉ kaḷattil kutittāṉ. paki maṟavarkaḷ avaṉi aṇukutaṟ kañci eṭṭa niṉṟu avaṉ mēl viṭṭa ampum, vēlum, avaṉ mēl niṟiyat tittaṉa. uyir uṭampi viṭṭuc ceṉṟu viṭṭatu. āṉāl, avaṉ uṭal nilattil viḻavilli. kāraṇam, ampum vēlum tēruruḷiṉ ārakkālkaḷ pōṉṟu uṭalil tittataṉāl, uṭal uruḷiṉ kuṭampōl nilattil paṭātu niṉṟatu. tēr - vaṇṭi, uruḷ - cakkaram, kuṭam - kumpam, eṉṉē maṟattiṉ māṇpu! attuṇi nēram kīḻ vīḻāmal poruta ammaṟavaṉatu maṟattiṉ perukku ettuṇiyō! maṟṟoru peruvīraṉ; māṟṟāṉ maṟavari maṇṇiṭip paṭuttup porutāṉ; muṭivil, tali kikālkaḷ aṟukkappaṭṭu vīḻntāṉ. aṟupaṭṭa taliyum, uṭalum, kiyum, kālum, aṭṭiyāṭutal pōla āṭikkoṇṭē iruntaṉa. attaku ciṟappuṭiyatu maṟam!
2. pōr vaki (puṟa - 17)
maṟavarkaḷ pakivar mēl ciṉaṅ koṇṭu poruvatēyaṉṟi, yāṉiyum, kutiriyum, tāmē ciṉaṅkoṇṭu porum. itu yāṉi maṟam, kutiri maṟam eṉappaṭum. tāṉi - maṟavar. maṟavar vīratti, maṟavar tāyar kūṟutal - mūtiṉ mulli, maṉiviyar kūṟutal - illāḷ mulli, kaṇṭōr kūṟutal - vallāṇ mulli eṉappaṭum. av yāṉiyum, kutiriyum, tamiḻ nāṭṭil piṟantu vaḷarntu tamiḻuri kēṭṭu, tamiḻaruṭaṉ paḻakiṉaviyallavā! vīrattukkeṉṉa kuṟiccal!
porunarum, porunarum, pulipōlap pukaṉṟu porutaṉar. oru paṭivēntaṉ muṉṉaṇiyiṟ ceṉṟu vēṟpōr purintaṉaṉ. avaṉi etirikaḷ cūḻntu koṇṭaṉar. atu kaṇṭa tāṉit talivaṉ oruvaṉ, virintu ceṉṟu etirikaḷit tākkip puṟaṅkaṇṭu tammaracaṉik kāttaṉaṉ. ituvaṉṟō vīrarkkaḻaku! itu tārnili eṉappaṭum.
ōriṭattē tāṉit talivar iruvar taṉi niṉṟu porutaṉar; avviruvarum taṉṉikarillāt taṉimaṟamikkōr; muṉippuṭaṉ iruvam ariyēṟupōl muṉintu porutaṉar. muṭivil iruvarum kaṇṭōr kaṇ kaluḻumpaṭi oruṅku maṭintaṉar. veṟṟi tōlvi eṉpaṉa aṉṉār tam maṟattiṉ perumi kaṇṭu aṇuka añciṉa pōlum!
maṟṟoru puṟam; oru paṭi oru paṭikku etir niṟka lāṟṟātu piṉ vāṅkic ceṉṟatu. taṉ paṭi piṉ vāṅkic celvatik kaṇṭa oru paṭittalivaṉ, taṉ paṭikku muṉ ceṉṟu, taṉ paṭiyit tākkiya etiri paṭiyit tākki erumikkaṭāp pōl muṉṉiṉṟu, taṉ paṭi ōṭāmaṟ kāttāṉ. ivaṉaṉṟō takutiyāṉa tāṉit talivaṉ!
oru maṟavaṉ etirikaḷi muṉintu tākkiṉāṉ; neṭunēram etirikaḷi veṭṭit taḷḷiṉāṉ. kuttik kuvittāṉ. ataṉāl, avaṉ vāḷum vēlum muṉimaḻuṅkip payaṉaṟṟup pōyiṉa. ampaṟāt tūṇiyum kāliyāyiṟṟu. paṭikkalam kiṭikkum variyilum uṭal valiyit tuṇayākak koṇṭu etirikaḷ tākkutaliṉiṉṟum taṉṉik kāttu vantāṉ. avaṉatu maṟac ciṟappiṉi avvetirikaḷē pārāṭṭiṉareṉil, tammavar pārāṭṭu talic collavā vēṇṭum! itu maṟattiṉ uccāṇi pōlum!
oru puṟam yāṉip pōr naṭantatu. paki vēntaṉ taṉ kaḷiṟṟiṉmiciyēṟik kaṟuttu mēl vantāṉ. kaḷiṟṟoṭu avaṉiyum koṉṟu kaḷittāṉ avaṉ ikal vēntaṉ. ak koṉṟa vēntaṉ maṟavarkaḷ, ak kollappaṭṭa vēntaṉic cūḻntu niṉṟu āṭiṉar. iḵtōr maṟakkuṟippākum. nam pōṉṟārkku iḵtōr nakiccuviyākat tōṉṟum. aṉṉārum nakiccuvi tōṉṟattāṉ avvāṟu ceytaṉar pōlum!
taṉ vēntaṉ pakivarkaḷāl kollappaṭṭatukaṇṭu oru perum paṭittalivaṉ, peruñciṉaṅ koṇṭu appakivarit tākkiṉāṉ. avaṉ avvāṟu tākkutalik kaṇṭa etiri paṭit talivaṉ oruvaṉ āṅkuṟṟu, avaṉiyum avaṉ paṭiyiyum nūḻilāṭṭiṉāṉ. nūḻil āṭṭal koṉṟu kuvittal. ivviru paṭittalivaril yār ciṟantavar? poṟuppuṇarcci, kaṭami yuṇarcci, naṉṉampikki eṉṟa mukkuṇaṅkaḷum avviruvariyum pukalaṭintaṉa pōlum!
oru pakkam vāṭpōr naṭantatu. atutāṉ apperum pōriṉ iṟutik kaṭṭam eṉattakum attaku kaṭumpōr atu. pōr toṭarntu naṭantatu. avvāṭpōril iru peru vēntarum etir niṉṟu porutoḻintaṉar. avvivarkkun tuṇivanta vēntarum avvāṟē porutoḻintaṉar. muṭivil iruperum paṭiyum porutu avvāṟē iṟantoḻintaṉa. eṉṉē pōriṉ koṭumi! itip pōriṉ koṭumi eṉpatā? vīrattiṉ neṭumi eṉpatā? yāteṉpatu! itaṉi,
“iruperu vēntarum tāmum cuṟṟamum
 oruvarum oḻiyāt tokinili”
eṉkiṉṟār āciriyar.
puṟattiṇi ēḻaṉuḷ veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi eṉṉum nāṉkum pōr muṟiyum; vāki, kāñci iraṇṭum vāḻkki muṟiyum; pāṭāṇ avviruvakiyum kūṟuvatākum.
nirikoṇṭōṉum nirikkuṭiyōṉum kaḷaṅkuṟittup porutal - veṭcip puṟattut tumpi eṉappaṭum maṇ vēṇṭic ceṉṟōṉum etirttōṉum porutal - vañcippuṟattut tumpi eṉappaṭum. akattuḻiñiyōṉukku vēṟōr vēntaṉ tuṇi vanta viṭattu, avaṉ puṟampōntu kaḷaṅkuṟittup porutal - uḻiñippuṟattut tumpi eṉappaṭum. iruvar eṅku poriṉum atu tumpi eṉappaṭum.
6. vāki (puṟa - 19)
kuṟiyāta muyaṟciyiyuṭiya ovvoruvarum tattam toḻi liyum oḻukkattiyum palapaṭiyākac ciṟappittuk kūṟutal - vāki eṉappaṭum. vāki - veṟṟi. ovvoru varum avaravar toḻil mutaliyavaṟṟil peṟum veṟṟiyic ciṟappittuk kūṟutal. pōr veṟṟiyum aṭaṅkum.
pārppār, aracar, vaṇikar, vuhḷar, aṟivar, antaṇar, vīrar eṉṉum eḻuvar toḻiṟ ciṟappum, pārppaṉar mutaliya avveḻuvar kīḻiruntu, aṉṉār toḻilkaḷip payilvōr toḻiṟ ciṟappum muṉṉar, (puṟa - 20) ‘toḻil’ eṉṉum talippil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itu nam muṉṉōrkaḷ toḻil muṉṉēṟṟat til, toḻil payilvatil evvaḷavu kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāka iruntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpataṟkut takka eṭuttukkāṭṭākum.
maṟamum aṟamum (puṟa - 21)
iṉi, vākit tiṇip potuvāka maṟattic ciṟappittum, aṟattic ciṟappittum kūṟum pakuti, paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uṭkōḷi appaṭiyē namakkup paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭuva toṉṟākum. nam muṉṉiyōr maṟavāḻviyum, aṟavāḻviyum nam kaṇmuṉ koṇṭu vantu niṟuttum pakuti ituvē. maṟam - vīram. itil maṟaveṉṟi oṉpatum aṟaveṉṟi oṉpatum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ makkaḷ vāḻkki muṟi aṉittum oruvāṟu tokuttuk kūṟappaṭutal pōṟṟaṟkuriyatākum.
1. maṟam : pōr ceyyac ceṉṟa - pakivayiṟ pirinta - talivaṉ, kūtirppācaṟi, vēṉiṟpācaṟi eṉṉum iru vakip pācaṟik kaṇṇum taṅkiyirukkum pōtu, taṭpaveṭpa mikunta akkālaṅ kaḷil, atāvatu puṇarccik kālamākiya kūtiriṉum, āṟu, kuḷam āṭal, kāvāṭal mutaliyavaṟṟāl iṉpuṟum kālamākiya vēṉiliṉum talivaṉ talivimēṟ kātaliṉṟip pōrviruppa muṭiyavaṉākavē iruppāṉ. itu pācaṟi veṉṟi eṉappaṭum. ivaṉ ettuṇip poṟuppuṭit talivaṉ!
kaḷavaḻi : tērēṟi vanta porunar mutaliyōr vēḷāḷar nellaṭikkum kaḷattē ceyyum ceykikaḷip pāṭutalallāmal, aracar āḷaṭikkum kaḷattē ceyyum ceykikaḷit tērēṟi vanta pulavar pāṭuvar. vēḷāḷar kaḷattil nelliyaṭittup paricilarkkuk koṭuppatupōla aracar pōri muṭittu eñciya pakivar, yāṉi kutiri mutaliyavaṟṟip paricilarkkuk koṭuppar.
ērkkaḷam pāṭutal, pōrkkaḷam pāṭutal eṉak kaḷam pāṭutal iruvakippaṭum. ērkkaḷattip porunar, pāṇar, mutaliyōr pāṭuvar. pōrkkaḷattip pulavar pāṭuvar. ērkkaḷa vaḻiyi uvamittup pōrkkaḷavaḻiyic ciṟappittu, nam muṉṉiyōr pōr veṉṟikku ēr veṉṟiyē kāraṇam eṉpati uṇarntu uḻavut toḻilik kaṇpōṟ karutip pōṟṟi vantami peṟappaṭum. itu kaḷaveṉṟi eṉappaṭum. poykiyār pāṭiyuḷḷa kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu itaṟku ilakkiyamākum. porunarum pāṇarum tērēṟic ceṉṟu kaḷampāṭiyiruttalāṉ, avarkaḷ aṉṟu maṟṟavarkaḷōṭu oppākavē matikkappaṭṭu vantami peṟappaṭum.
tērvēntar palariyum veṉṟa vēntaṉ, avveṟṟik kaḷippāl, paṭittalivarōṭu kikōttuk koṇṭu tērt taṭṭiṉkaṇ kuraviyāṭuvāṉ. itu muṉṟērk kuravi eṉappaṭum. tēriṉ piṉṉē maṟavarkaḷ kuraviyāṭuvar. itu piṉṟērk kuravi eṉappaṭum. kuravi palar kikōttāṭutal. itu peṇkaḷ āṭum kūttuvakiyuḷ oṉṟu. makaḷir āṭum kuravik kūtti veṟṟik kaḷippāl maṉṉaṉum maṟṟavarum āṭutalāṉ itu veṉṟiyāyiṟṟu.
pakivar palarum aṇuka añci akalumpaṭi oruvaṉ vēṟ ṟoḻilil tērcci peṟuvatu vēlveṉṟi eṉappaṭum. vāḷ veṉṟiyum,  vil veṉṟiyum itil aṭaṅkumāki yāl itu paṭiveṉṟi eṉappaṭum. itaṉāl, aṉṟu ovvoru toḻiliṉum taṉippayiṟci peṟṟa vallunarkaḷ (Expert) iruntu, avvattoḻili mēṉmēlum muṉṉēṟṟa muṟac ceytu vantaṉar eṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
iṉi, oruvaṉ oruvar iruvarōṭu etirppatu iyaṟki. oruvaṉ ciṟitum añcātu, oru perumpaṭi muṉ taṉiyākac ceṉṟu etirttupa poruvatu, apperumpaṭiyit tōṟṟōṭum paṭi ceyvatu iyaṟkikku appāṟpaṭṭa ceyalākum. attakiya vallunarum aṉṟiruntaṉar. itu maṟaveṉṟi eṉappaṭum. porutta māṉa peyar!
niliyillāta uyirvāḻkkiyi virumpātu, niliyāṉa maṟappukaḻi virumputalum maṟaveṉṟiyēyākum. ittakiya maṟattaṉmiyē aṉṟu tamiḻnāṭu vīrattiṉ uṟiviṭamāka iruntamikkuk kāraṇamākum.
oru maṟavaṉ, ‘nāṉ iṉṉa nērattiṟkuḷ ippaṭiyi vellēṉeṉil, tīyil vīḻntiṟappēṉ’ eṉa vañciṉaṅ kūṟip porutāṉ. avvāṟu vañciṉaṅ kūṟip poruta avaṉ. annērat tiṟkuḷ appaṭiyi vella muṭiyātu pōkavē. tāṉ kūṟiya vañciṉan tappātu; pakivar nāṇa, periyōrkaḷ pārāṭṭa, tī vaḷarttu atil vīḻntu uyir tuṟantāṉ. eṉṉē avvīraṉiṉ coṟṟavaṟāmi! itu colveṉṟi eṉappaṭum. maṟavariṉ ittakiya vañciṉamē maṟatti vaḷarttu, vaṉkaṇmiyit tantu,  ūkkamum, āḷviṉiyum oruṅkamiyac ceytu aṉṉārkku veṟṟip pukaḻit tantateṉka.
pakivarēṉum, avar cuṟṟattārēṉum vēṇṭum utaviyi maṟukkāmaṟ ceytalum maṟacceyalēyākum. atāvatu, pōr naṭakkum pōtu ciṟanta paṭiyēvāmi, pōric caṟṟunēram niṟuttivittal pōṉṟa utaviyi vēṇṭiṉ pakivariṉ av vēṇṭukōṭku icital. itu pakivarkku ākkantaruva teṉiṉum maṟattaṉmiyāl icitalākum. itu tuṇiveṉṟi eṉappaṭum.
“pērāṇmi yeṉpa taṟukaṇoṉ ṟuṟṟakkāl
 ūrāṇmi maṟṟata ṉeḵku.” (kuṟaḷ)
eṉṟa kuṟaḷ ikkaruttuṭiyatē.
ūrāṇmi - utavi. ivvoṉpatum pōr paṟṟiyaṉavē yākalāṉ maṟacceyalkaḷāyiṉa.
2. aṟam
uḻavarkaḷ eruttiyum, erumiyiyum koṇṭu uḻavañ cāmal uḻutoḻil ceytu nāṭṭumakkaḷik kāttu varukiṉṟaṉar. maṟavarkaḷ yāṉiyiyum, kutiriyiyum koṇṭu pōrañcātu pōrttoḻil ceytu nāṭṭumakkaḷik kāttu varukiṉṟaṉar. muṉivar paci varuttāmalum, piṉṉavar paki varuttāmalum kākkiṉṟaṉar. ivviru toḻililum veṟṟi peṟṟa vaḻiyē nāṭṭumakkaḷi naṉkiṉitu kākka muṭiyumākiyāl, itu toḻil veṉṟiyākum. paciyum pakiyum makkaḷi varuttik kollum pāṉmiya vākaliṉ, avviraṇṭu muṟāmal makkaḷik kāppaṉa uḻavum pōrumēyākum. pōr, vīrac ceyalik kuṟikkum. ittakiya karuttamiya avviru toḻiliyum iṇittuk kūṟiya āciriyariṉ matinuṭpam viyattaṟkuriya toṉṟākum.
muṟiyāka aracu purintu vanta ōr aracaṉ, niliyāmiyi eṇṇi, araci iḷavaracuk kīntu amitiyuṭaṉ iruppāṉ. araca niliyit tuṟappatu aritākaliṉ, itu aracaveṉṟi eṉappaṭum. araci nītta ivaṉ potunalañ ceyyappukiṉ, antaṇaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ aracamarapiliruntu tōṉṟiya antaṇaṉ, ‘antaṇar’ eṉṉum talippip pārkka.
aṟaṅkūṟaviyam eṉpatu, iṉṟuḷḷa uyarmuṟi maṉṟam (Highcourt) pōṉṟatākum. naṭuvar, amiccar mutaliyōr avva viyattiṉ uṟuppiṉarāvar. kuṭippiṟappu, kalvi, oḻukkam, vāymi, tūymi, naṭunilimi, aḻukkāṟiṉmi, avāviṉmi eṉṉum eṇ kuṇaṅkaḷum amiyap peṟṟa cāṉṟōrkaḷē avvaviyat tārākat takutiyuṭiyavarāvar. aṉṉār aṟaṅ kūṟuvatil - vaḻakkut tīrppatil - ciṟantu viḷaṅkuvar. atu aviyaveṉṟi eṉappaṭum. karikālaṉ narimuṭittu aṟaṅkūṟiya ceytiyi aṟika. eṇ kuṇaṅkaṭkum ilakkiyam tirukkuṟaḷiṟ kāṇka.
makkaḷ illaṟa voḻukkaṅkaḷil tavaṟātu naṭattal, ciṟanta koṭikkuṇamuṭiyarāy iruttal. mikka poṟumikkuṇa muṭiyarātal, poruḷillārk kivvulaka iṉpam illiyāta lāl (kuṟaḷ - 247). poruḷīṭṭuvatil perumuyaṟciyuṭiyarātal ākiya innāṉkum muṟiyē oḻukkaveṉṟi, koṭi veṉṟi, poṟi veṉṟi, poruḷ veṉṟi eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟiṟku ilakkiyam tirukkuṟaḷiṟ kāṇka. ivvaṟac ceyalkaḷ nāṉkum illaṟattārk kuriyavi. aṭutta iraṇṭum tuṟavaṟattārkkuriyavi.
illaṟatti nīttār mikka aruḷuḷḷamuṭiyarākavum, poruṭ paṟṟillātavarākavum iruttal vēṇṭum. aruḷuṭi miyiṉum, paṟṟiṉmiyiṉum ciṟantiruttal tuṟavuveṉṟi yākum. ilakkiyam tirukkuṟaḷiṟ kāṇka.
pallāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē iṅṅaṉam vāḻkki muṟikaḷi variyaṟi ceytu,avaṟṟil ciṟantu - veṟṟiyuṭaṉ - vāḻavēṇṭumeṉa ilakkaṇam ceytu vāḻnta perumi tamiḻarkkē yuriya taṉipperumiyākum.
7. kāñci (puṟa - 23)
kāñci eṉpatu - niliyāmi. celvam  niliyāmi, iḷami niliyāmi, yākki niliyāmi eṉa niliyāmi mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. ulakiyaṟ poruḷāṉa aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpaṅkaḷi nukara muṭiyāmaṟ ceyvaṉa inniliyāmikaḷē yākalāṉ, anniliyāmiyi yuṇarntāl tāṉ aṟam poruḷ iṉpaṅkaḷiṉ payaṉic cariyāṉapaṭi aṭiya muṭiyum. niliyāmiyiṉi yuṇarātār aṟam poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷic cariyāṉa paṭi nukara muṭiyātu. aṟam poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷi aḷavaṟintu nukarum poruṭṭu annili yāmikaḷic cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatu kāñcit tiṇiyākum.
ati āṇpāṟ kāñci, peṇpāṟ kāñci eṉa iru kūṟu ceytu kūṟum āciriyariṉ ulakiyalaṟivu aṟintu iṉpuṟaṟpāla toṉṟākum. avviru kūṟum āṇpeṇ irupālukkum potu vēṉum, ciṟappuṭimi nōkki iru kūṟākap pakuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. potuviyum, ciṟappiyum aṟintu ataṟkēṟpak koḷvatu namatu kaṭamiyākum. āṇpāṟ kāñci - āṭavariṉ niliyāmi. peṇpāṟ kāñci - peṇṭiriṉ niliyāmi.
1. āṇpāṟ kāñci (puṟa - 24)
piṟarāl taṭuttaṟkariya kūṟṟam varumeṉap periyōr kūṟuvatu - peruṅ kāñci eṉappaṭum. uṭalum, uyirum kūṟupaṭum kālatti - cāviṉi - kūṟṟu, kūṟṟam, kūṟṟuvaṉ eṉpatu nūṉmarapu. veṟṟiyi - koṟṟavi eṉpatu pōla (puṟa - 4. 1.6)
itu yākki niliyāmi. yākki niliyāmiyuṇarntāl tāṉ, yākkiyiṉ payaṉāṉa aṟam poruḷ iṉpaṅ kōṭalil ārvamum, payaṉ koṇṭa yākkiyiṉ aḻivukku añcāmiyum uṇṭākum. itaṟku ilakkiyam nālaṭiyār. yākki niliyāmi eṉṉum atikārattiṟ kāṇka.
iḷami kaḻinta mutiyōr, avviḷami kaḻiyātārkku - iḷiyōrkku - avviḷami niliyāmiyik kūṟuvatu - mutukāñci eṉappaṭum. itu iḷami niliyāmi. iḷami niliyāmi yuṇarntāṟṟāṉ, avviḷamip paruvattiṟ ceyya virumpum oḻukkak kēṭāṉa ceyalkaḷic ceyyāmaṟ ceyyum. itaṟkum ilakkiyam nālaṭiyār. iḷami niliyāmi eṉṉum atikārattiṟ kāṇka.
uyir vāḻkki niliyāmiyuṭiya teṉpati ārāyn taṟintu, pōril paṭṭapuṇṇik kiḻittuk koṇṭu uyir viṭṭup pukaḻ koḷḷutal - maṟakkāñci eṉappaṭum. appuṇṇi āṟṟik koṇṭu vāḻiṉum oru nāḷikku iṟantē tīravēṇṭu mākiyāl, atukāṟum kāttirāmal appuṇṇik kiḻittuk koṇṭu uyirviṭuvar eṉpatām. itu yākki niliyāmi. eṉṉē nam muṉṉiyōriṉ uṇmiyuṇarcciyiṉ ūṟṟam!
pōrkkaḷattil kāppōriṉṟip puṇpaṭṭu iṟantu kiṭap pavaṉi uyiruḷḷavaṉāka eṇṇi, accattiṉāl naḷḷiravil nari mutaliya uyirkaḷ avaṉi aṇukā takalvatu -pēykkāñci eṉappaṭum. pēy - accam. nāy nari tiṉṉāmaṟ kiṭantatu avaṉ piṇam. cuṟṟattāriṉmiyāl, itu celvaniliyāmiyāyiṟṟu, nariverūuttaliyār eṉṉum pulavariṉ peyarkkuṟippāl, nāṟ nari kaṇṭañcum attaku niliyiṟ kiṭantāṉeṉṟumām.
oruvaṉ iṟantapōtu, ‘avaṉ ittaṉmiyaṉ’ eṉak kūṟip palarum iraṅkuvar. itu perumpālum avaṉ ceyta naṉmikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟi, iṉi attakiya naṉmikaḷ ceytal kaḻintatē - tavirntatē - eṉa iraṅkuvatāl, itu maṉṉik kāñci eṉappaṭum. maṉ-kaḻivupporuḷ kuṟikkum iṭiccol, atiyamāṉ iṟanta pōtu oḷaviyār iraṅkip pāṭiya puṟappāṭṭu (235) itaṟku ilakkiyamākum. itu yākki niliyāmi. itaṉāl paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṉ potunalap paṇpāṭu iṉitu viḷaṅkum.
‘iṉṉatu ceyyat tavaṟiṉāl iṉṉatu āvēṉ’ eṉak kūṟiya vañciṉattil tappātatu - vañciṉakkāñci eṉappaṭum. muṉ vākit tiṇiyil (puṟa- 22) maṟavaṉ kūṟiya vañciṉam vīram paṟṟiyatu. itu niliyāmi paṟṟiyatu. ‘ciṉaṅkeḻu vēntarit tākki muracaṅ koḷḷēṉāyiṉ, kuṭipaḻi tūṟṟuṅ kōlēṉākuka, pulavar pāṭātu varika veṉ nilavari’ eṉat taliyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ kūṟiya vañciṉam (puṟam - 72) itaṟku ilakkiyamākum. ‘uṉṉi muntāviṭṭāl uṉ peyari eṉakku vi.’ ‘intat taṭavi tavaṟiṉāl oru pakkattu mīciyi eṭuttuk koḷkiṟēṉ’ eṉpaṉavum itaṉ pāṟpaṭum.
pakivar paṭikkalañ ceyta pimpuṇṇi aṇi kalaṉākap peṟṟu iṟantu kiṭakkum taṉ kaṇavaṉatu uṭalit toṭuvataṟ kañci, naḷḷiravil maṉivi toṭātu kāttu varuvatu - toṭāk kāñci eṉappaṭum. uḷaṅkalanta aṉpuṭik kātaliyē toṭāta nilimi eytiyataṉāl, itu āṇpāṟ kāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl, paḻantamiḻ makaḷir pōrkkaḷañ ceṉṟu, āṭavar ceyyum pōrc ceyali nēril kaṇṭu kaḷitta maṟakkuṇamuṭiyar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ammaṟakkuṭi makaḷir peṟṟa maṟavarkaḷallarō paḻantamiḻakattip pātukāttu vantaṉar!
uyir nītta kaṇavaṉatu uṭampiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa vēluṇ ṭākkiya puṇkaḷik kaṇṭu maṉivi añcuvatu - āñcik kāñci eṉappaṭum. ‘vaḷiyiṭi pōḻap paṭātu muyaṅkiya’ (kuṟaḷ - 1108) maṉiviyē am mārpiṉikkaṇṭañcumpaṭi attaku puṇṇuṟap poruta avaṉ maṟamē maṟam! ivviraṇṭum yākki niliyāmi; iṉpa niliyāmiyum koḷka.
taṉ makaḷik koṭukka maṟuttamiyāl, valitiṟ koḷḷa vanta maṉṉaṉukku avaḷik koṭukka iciyātu, avaṉōṭu pōr ceyyat tuṇital - makaṭpāṟ kāñci eṉappaṭum. itu, kātaliṉṟi valitiṟ kōṭal takāteṉṉum maṟak kuṟippu. itu iḷami niliyāmi. itaṉāl, paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ kaḷavoḻukkat ti - kātaṟ kalappi - ettakiya mēmpāṭuṭiyatāka matittup pōṟṟi vantaṉar eṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṟatallavā? itu, piṟkālattē kalampakattiṉ uṟuppukkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa maṟam eṉak koḷḷap paṭṭatu.
iṟanta kaṇavaṉ taliyit taḻuviyapaṭiyē maṉivi uṭaṉuyir nīttal - taliyoṭu muṭital eṉappaṭum. itu aṉpum, yākkiyum, oruṅku niliyāmi. irāvaṇaṉ iṟantatum vaṇṭār kuḻali uṭaṉuyir nīttatum, pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ aracu kaṭṭilil tuñciyatum avaṉ kōpperuntēvi uṭaṉuyir viṭṭatum itaṟku ilakkiyamākum.
iḷamip paruvattē āṇpeṇ irupālārkkum innili yāmikaḷiyellām viḷakkamāka eṭutturittu vantāl, iḷiñarkaḷ tīya oḻukkaṅkaḷik kaṉavilēṉuṅ karutuvārkaḷā? īyāttaṉmi avarkaḷi ekkāraṇaṅ koṇṭum aṇuka muṭiyumā? piṟari ēmāṟṟutal, kaḷḷattaṉam, iṉṉā ceytal mutaliya keṭṭa kuṇaṅkaḷ kiṭṭumā? māṟāka, makkaḷiṉam nalloḻukkam eṉṉum ellik kōṭṭukkuḷ iṉitu cellumaṉṟō? āṉāl, itiyellām yār eṇṇip pārkkiṟārkaḷ? vāḻkkikkup puṟampāṉatum keṭṭavaḻikku iṭṭuc celvatumāṉa ceytikaḷ tāmē iṉṟu makkaṭku vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟu tamiḻp perumakkaṭkut tamiḻuṇarcciyuṇṭākumō!
2. peṇpāṟ kāñci
perum pukaḻoṭu iṟantā ṉoruvaṉip peṇṭir palar cūḻntu niṉṟu āravārattuṭaṉ aḻutal - pūcal mayakkam eṉappaṭum. iṉṟu iṟantāric cūḻntu oppāri pāṭip peṇkaḷ aḻum vaḻakkam paṇṭiya vaḻakkamē eṉpatu aṟintu iṉpuṟaṟ pāla toṉṟaṉṟō? peṇkaḷ pāṭiyaḻum aḻukiṟa pāṭṭukkaḷi yellām tokuttu, avaṟṟiluḷḷa karuttukkaḷip piḻinteṭut tāl, atu makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ varaṉmuṟip paṭappiṭippāka irukkum. attaku poruḷ potintavi appāṭalkaḷ. avi peṇ makkaḷiṉ uḷakkōḷuṇarcciyāl uruppeṟṟaviyākum. paḻan tamiḻp peṇṭirkaḷiṉ pūcalmayakkap pāṭalkaḷ ettakiyaṉavō!
kaṇṭōr poṟuttaṟ kariya peruntuṉpaṅ koḷḷum paṭi, cuṟṟattāriṉṟi, maṉivi taṉiyāka iṟanta kaṇavaṉiṭattiruntaḻuvatu - tāṅkarumpiyuḷ eṉappaṭum. piyuḷ - tuṉpam. attaṉi niliyi eṅṅaṉam tāṅka muṭiyum!
kaṇavaṉ iṟantatum, maṉiviyum uṭaṉuyir nīttatik kaṇṭōr piṟarukkuk kūṟutal - mūtāṉantam eṉappaṭum. āṉantam - cākkāṭu. muṉ taṉittiruntaḻutavaḷē iṟantaṉaḷ pōlum!
koṭiya curattiṉ kaṇ kaṇavaṉi iḻanta orutti, taṉ taṉimiyik kūṟiyaḻutal - mutupāli eṉappaṭum. pāli - pirivu. mutupāli - perum pirivu. curam - ariyavaḻi. kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum veḷiyūr cellum pōtu nērvatu itu. uṭaṉ pōkkil nērtalum kūṭum. ivi nāṉkum iṉpamum, celvamum, oruṅku niliyāmi.
kaṇavaṉōṭu maṉiviyum uṭaṉuyir nītta viṭattu, avarkaḷ muṉ puṉalāṭiyum, poḻilāṭiyum iṉpuṟṟa niliyi āyattārum piṟarum piṟarkkuk kūṟutal - kiyaṟunili eṉappaṭum. itu celva niliyāmi.
ulakam evvaḷavu piṇippukkuṭ paṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟatu! ippiṇippu illiyēl, iṉṟu ivvulakam maṇṇulakamākat tāṉ irukkum. oruvark koruvarkkuḷḷa toṭarpu tāṉ eṉṉa! kātal, aṉpu, naṭpu, paṟṟu, pācam eṉṉum coṟporuḷiṉ perumitāṉeṉṉavō!
“paṇpuṭiyārp paṭṭuṇ ṭulakam atuviṉṟēl
 maṇpukku māyvatu maṉ.” (kuṟaḷ)
eṉpatu vaḷḷuvar vāymoḻi.
iṉṟu kimmikaḷ maṭṭum iruppatu pōlaṉṟi aṉṟu kimmarum iruntaṉar. āciriyar, kimmi niliyi - kātalaṉ iḻanta tāpata nili eṉavum, kimmaṉ niliyi - kātali iḻanta taputāra nili eṉavum kūṟukiṉṟār. ittāpata niliyum, taputāra niliyum yākkiyum, iṉpamum oruṅku niliyāmiyākum. maṉiviyiṟanta piṉ iraṇṭāntāram koḷḷak karutiṉ kāñciyākātu.
itaṉāl, nam muṉṉiyōr iṉṟupōl iṉpan tuykkavum, piḷḷi peṟavum, cōṟākkavum tammi maṅkalaṉākkavum pala tāraṅ koḷḷum vaḻakkattik kikkoṇṭirukka villiyeṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. oru cila aṭaṅkāmāṟikaḷit tavira maṟṟellōrum kaḷavoḻukka moḻuki maṇantu koṇṭa maṉiviyiṟakkiṉ, taputāra niliyilēyē iruntuvantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
āṇkaḷukku aṉṟu taputāra niliyi nīkkik koḷḷum - maṟumaṇañ ceytu koḷḷum - urimi iruntirukkum pōtu, peṇkaḷukkum aṉṟu tāpata niliyi nīkkik koḷḷum urimi iruntirukkalām. āṉāl, atip paṟṟiya kuṟippoṉṟum kāṇavilli. tāpatanilikku - ‘kimmi’ eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkuc col iruppatupōla, taputāra nilikku ulaka vaḻakkuc col iṉmiyāl - ‘kimmaṉ’ eṉṉum col vaḻakkil iṉmiyāl - piṟkālattē āṇmakkaḷ, peṇmakkaḷ piṟappurimiyip paṟittu viṭṭami peṟappaṭum. paḻantamiḻ oḻukkamākiya taputāra niliyi āṇmakkaḷ iyaṉṟa maṭṭilum kaṭippiṭippatu. āṇ peṇ orumi vāḻvukkum, nalloḻukka nikaḻvukkum ēṟṟatākum.
kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ uṭaṉeripukac celvati vilakkuvati maṟuttup peṇkaḷ uṭaṉeripukutalum uṇṭu. itu pālinili eṉappaṭum. pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyārāṉa peruṅkōppeṇṭu eṉṉum peṇṇaraciyār, avvāṟu taṭuttōri ikaḻntu kūṟi maṟuttuc ceṉṟu uṭaṉeripukkami 246 puṟappāṭṭāl terikiṟatu.
kaṇavaṉiṟantatum uṭaṉuyir viṭutal - taliyaṉpu eṉavum, uṭaṉeri pukutal - iṭiyaṉpu eṉavum, tāpatanili - kaṭiyaṉpu eṉavum paṭum. tāmē etirppaṭṭuk kaḷavoḻukka moḻukik kātal mutirntu maṇañceytu koṇṭa akkālap peṇkaṭkut taliyaṉpum, iṭiyaṉpum uṇmiyākavē iruntirukkalām. āṉāl, kaṭṭāya maṇam puriyum ikkālap peṇkaḷil oru cilarit tavira maṟṟavarkaḷukkuk kaṭiyaṉpu kūṭa irukkak kāraṇa milli. mēlum, āṭavari viṭap peṇṭir mikavum iḷakiya uḷḷamuṭiyavarāvar. atuvē avarkaḷ eḷitil uyiri māyttuk koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamākum.
ivvāṟu āṇkaḷukkāka uyirviṭum peṇṇulakat tit tāṉ āṇkaḷ oru vilippaṇṭam pōla eṇṇi, avvāṟē naṭattiyum varukiṉṟaṉar. vāḻkkiyiṉ cari paṅkāḷikaḷāṉa peṇkaḷiṉ piṟappurimiyi vaḻaṅka maṟuppatu āṇkaḷiṉ taṉṉalamē kāraṇamākum.
tam maṟakkuṭippukaḻ keṭutaluk kētuvākat tam makaṉ pōril puṟaṅkoṭuttāṉ eṉak kēṭṭa tāyar ammāṉak kēṭṭip poṟātu uyirviṭuvar. makaṉip pōrukkaṉuppi viṭṭu, avaṉ perumiyi aṟiyak kāttirunta tāyar, avaṉ veṟṟiyuṭaṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉpatik kēṭṭatum makiḻcciyuṭaṉ uyirviṭuvar. ivviraṇṭum talippeyal nili eṉappaṭum. ittakiya maṟakkuṭi makaḷir iruntāl iṉṟu tamiḻnāṭu in niliyilā irukkum? eṉṟu tamiḻnāṭu attakiya maṟakkuṭi makaḷiriyum, mintariyum peṟṟu māṇpuṭaṉ viḷaṅkumō!
muṭivāka ulakil piṟantōrellām iṟantu pōkavum, eṉṟum niliyākavuḷḷa puṟaṅkāṭṭi vāḻtti amitiyuṟuvar. itu kāṭu vāḻttu eṉappaṭum.
vāḻkkiyil nērum palavakit tuṉpaṅkaḷiyum viri vākavum, viḷakkamākavum eṭuttuk kūṟumukattāṉ, niliyāmi eṉṉum uṇmiyi yuḷḷapaṭi yuṇarntu, vāḻkkiyil veṟuppuk koḷḷāmal amintu vāḻavēṇṭum eṉpatē kāñcit tiṇiyiṉ karuttākum.
8. pāṭāṇ
pāṭāṇ tiṇiyāvatu - koṭiyāḷarip pulavarkaḷ pukaḻntu pāṭuvatu. pāṭu āṇ tiṇi - pāṭappaṭum āṇ makaṉatu oḻukkam. atāvatu, pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉiṉ vīram, koṭi pukaḻ mutaliya oḻukkattic ciṟappittup pāṭutal pāṭāṇṭiṇi eṉappaṭum. pāṭutaliyum, pāṭappaṭutaliyum oru tiṇiyāka amitta nam muṉṉiyōr matinuṭpamē nuṭpam!
atu, veṭci mutaliya ēṉi āṟutiṇikaḷiyum pāṭutal, taṉakkē uriya poruḷip pāṭutal eṉa iruvakippaṭum. veṭci mutaliya tiṇikaḷip pāṭutal - pāṭāṇṭalivaṉ veṭci mutaliya tiṇicceyalkaḷ nikaḻttiyatākap pāṭutal. taṉakkē uriya poruḷip pāṭutal - pāṭāṇṭiṇikkē uriya poruḷip pāṭutal. atu, tēvarppāṭāṇ, makkaṭpāṭāṇ eṉa iru vakippaṭum. eṉavē,
1. veṭcip pāṭāṇ	5. vākip pāṭāṇ
2. vañcippāṭāṇ	6. kāñcip pāṭāṇ
3. uḻiñip pāṭāṇ	7. tēvarp pāṭāṇ
4. tumpip pāṭāṇ	8. makkaṭ pāṭāṇ
eṉa, appāṭāṇ eṭṭu vakippaṭum (puṟa - 22). veṭcip pāṭāṇ - pāṭāṇṭiṇip poruḷōṭu veṭcit tiṇip poruḷum amiyap pāṭutal. ēṉiyavum ivvāṟē.
makkaḷi uyarttit tēvarākkip pāṭutalum, uyar poruḷ kaḷi uyarttup pāṭutalum - tēvarppāṭāṇ ākum. makkaḷi makkaḷākavē pāṭutal - makkaṭ pāṭāṇ ākum. tēvarip pāṭutal - tēvarp pāṭāṇpāṭṭu eṉavum, makkaḷip pāṭutal - cen tuṟip pāṭāṇpāṭṭu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum (puṟa - 27). pāṭappaṭum poruḷi ivvāṟu iyalpākavum uyarvuṭiyatākavum paṭittuk koṇṭu pāṭum paḻantamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ māṇpē māṇpu!
pāṭāṇ poruḷ - paraval, pukaḻcci, kāmam eṉa mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. paraval - vāḻttal. kāmam - puṇarcci vēṭki.
teyvap pakuti - vāḻttal, puṇarcci vēṭki eṉa iru poruḷ peṟum.
makkaṭpakuti - vāḻttal, pukaḻtal, puṇarcci vēṭki eṉa mupporuḷ peṟum.
1. tēvarp pāṭāṇ
amarkaṇ muṭiyum aṟuvakiyāṉum - tēvarkaṭ poruntum aṟumuṟi vāḻttum, amararkaṇ muṭiyum, puritīr kāmam pulliya vakiyiṉum - kuṟṟan tīrnta puṇarcci vēṭkiyum - tēvarp pakutiyākum (puṟa - 26)
1. vāḻttal
vāḻttalāvatu - oṉṟaṉ ciṟappiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟutal. avvāḻttu - aṟumuṟi vāḻttu, ēṉivāḻttu eṉa iru vakippaṭum.
aṟumuṟi vāḻttāvaṉa - vāṉ, nīttār, aṟam, āṉ, aracaṉ, ulaku eṉṉum āṟiyum vāḻttutal. iviyāṟum ciṟanta poruḷkaḷākiyāl teyvaṅkaḷāyiṉa. mutal mūṉṟu vāḻttum tirukkuṟaḷil vantuḷḷami kāṇka. pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉi vāḻttum pōtu, uyarnta poruḷkaḷiyum uṭaṉ vāḻttutal pāṭāṇṭiṇi marapākum. ippāṭāṇṭiṇit teyva viḷak kattit ‘teyvam’ eṉṉum talippiṟ kāṇka.
ivvaṟumuṟi vāḻttip pōla, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, uyar kuṇam ākiya mūṉṟiyum vāḻttutalum uṇṭu. ivi muṟiyē koṭinili, vaḷḷi, kantaḻi eṉappaṭum (puṟa - 33). koṭinili - kīḻtticik kaṇṇē tōṉṟum ñāyiṟu. koṭi - kīḻttici. nili - iṭam. vaḷḷi - vaṇmiyuṭiyatu - tiṅkaḷ. veṅkatir pōl kāyāmal, taṇkatir tantu makiḻvippatāl tiṅkaḷ - vaḷḷi eṉappaṭṭatu. kantaḻi kuṇam. kantu - kuṟi. aḻi - aḻintatu - aṟṟa. kantaḻi kuṟiyaṟṟatu. kuṟi - kuṇi - poruḷ. kuṟiyaṟṟatu - kuṇam centāmari eṉpatil, cemmi - kuṇam. tāmari - kuṇi kuṇattiyuṭiyatu - kuṇi. niṟam cuvi vaṭivu aḷavu taṉmi eṉṉum aivakip paṇpum (ecca - 20) poruḷōṭu uṭaṉṟōṉṟi, poruḷ aḻiyum pōtu uṭaṉaḻivatāl, kuṇam - kantaḻi eṉap paṭṭatu. paṇpu - kuṇam. vāḷā ‘ceyki’ eṉṟāl, ‘naṟceyki’ eṉap poruḷ paṭutal pōla, kuṇam eṉpatu uyarkuṇattik kuṟikkum. uyar kuṇam, vāṉ, nīttār, aṟam eṉṉum nāṉkum tirukkuṟaḷil mutal nāṉku atikāraṅkaḷil vāḻttappaṭutal kāṇka. ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum cilappatikāra mutaṟpāṭṭil3 vāḻttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
2. puṇarcci vēṭki
kaṭavuḷ makkaṭ peṇṭiri nayattal, kaṭavuḷi makkaṭ peṇṭir nayattal eṉap puṇarcci vēṭki iruvakippaṭum (puṟa - 28). nayattal - virumputal. aracarkaḷē pāṭāṇṭiṇiyil teyva māka uyarttip pāṭappaṭuvatāl, ōr aracaṉi orutti virumpuva tākavum, ōr aracaṉ oruttiyi virumpuvatākavum pāṭutalē puṇarcci vēṭki eṉappaṭum. ōr aracaṉi oru peṇ virumpu vatākap pāṭutal - kaṭavuḷi makkaṭ peṇṭir nayattal eṉappaṭum. ōr aracaṉ oruttiyi virumpuvatākap pāṭutal - kaṭavuḷ makkaṭ peṇṭiri nayattal eṉappaṭum. araca kaṉṉiyar muṭimaṉṉarukku maṉiviyarāṉa piṉṉarē teyvamāka uyarcci peṟuvārākiyāl, kaṭavuṭ peṇṭiri makkaḷ nayattalākap pāṭutal illi. itaṉāṉum, paṇṭu tamiḻ makkaḷukkuḷ piṟavi vēṟṟumi illā tiruntami peṟappaṭum. ippakuti makkaṭ kalappuṭiya teyvap pakutiyākumām.
2. makkaṭ pāṭāṇ
makkaḷi vāḻttiyum pukaḻntum pāṭutalum, aṟam poruḷiṉpam paṟṟip pāṭutalum - centuṟip pāṭāṇ pāṭṭu eṉappaṭum (puṟa - 27). centuṟiyāvatu - makkaḷi makkaḷākavē pāṭutal. patiṟṟup pattum, puṟanāṉūṟṟil varum pāṭāṇṭiṇip pāṭṭukkaḷum makkaḷip pukaḻntu pāṭum centuṟip pāṭāṇ pāṭṭukku ilakkiyamākum. tirukkuṟaḷ, aṟam poruḷiṉpam paṟṟi pāṭum centuṟip pāṭāṇ pāṭṭukku ilakkiyamākum.
1. piḷḷip pāṭṭu
peṟṟōrkkup piḷḷiyiṉpāl uḷḷa aṉpi viṭa aṉpukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭu vēṟoṉṟum illi. attakiya aṉpu avvaṉpu! ittakiya aṉpukkuriya piḷḷiyic ciṟappittup pāṭip peṟṟōri makiḻvittup paricu peṟṟu vanta paḻantamiḻp pulavarkaḷiṉ pulamit tiṟaṉ aṟintu iṉpuṟaṟ pāla toṉṟākum. oru talivaṉiṭam paricu peṟac ceṉṟa pulavaṉ, attalivaṉatu piḷḷiyic ciṟappittup pāṭutal. piḷḷip pāṭṭu eṉappaṭum (puṟa - 29). appiḷḷi antantap paruvaṅkaḷiṟ ceyyum ceyalic ciṟappittu. ataṉ muṉṉōriṉ pukaḻuṭaṉ cērttup pāṭappaṭum (puṟa - 35). muṉṉōriṉ vīram, koṭi, pukaḻ mutaliyavi appiḷ ḷiyiṉavākap pāṭutal. itil āṇ peṇ irupāṟ kuḻaviyum aṭaṅkum. piḷḷiyic ciṟappittup pāṭip peṟṟōri makiḻvittup paricu peṟum vaḻakkam aruki, piṟkālattē talivar kaḷiyē piḷḷipparuvattiṉarākkip pāṭum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu. itu, piḷḷittamiḻ eṉa 96 vakip paṉuvalkaḷil oṉṟāṉatu.
iṉi, oru piḷḷi teruvil viḷiyāṭum pōtu, ūr makaḷir appiḷḷiyiṉ naṭi, vaṭivu, aḻaku, kutali mutaliya vaṟṟi viyantu, ataṉ muṉṉōr ceyaluṭaṉ cērttup pukaḻva tākap pāṭutalum uṇṭu (puṟa - 30). ituvum piḷḷip pāṭṭē yākum. ituvē piṟkālēttē, ōr aracaṉ ūr valam varum pōtu, pēti, petumpi, mutaliya ēḻparuvap peṇṭirum virumpuvatākap pāṭum ulācceyyuḷāyiṟṟu. immāṟṟam, piṟkālat talivarkaḷiṉ taṟperumit taṉmiyāl uṇṭāṉatākum.
2. vāḻttal (puṟa - 35)
koṭuppōri vāḻttik koṭārit tāḻttik kūṟutal paḻantamiḻp pulavarkaḷiṉ iyalpu. ‘illōr irappatum iyalpē; iyalpē irappōrk kīvatum uṭiyōr kaṭaṉē’ eṉa, uḷḷōr kaṭamiyi viḷaṅkak kūṟi, ‘celvattup payaṉē ītal’ eṉṉum celvattiṉ payaṉpāṭṭic celvarkaḷ aṟiyumpaṭi ceytu, nāṭṭil oppuravuṇṭākkutalik kaṭamiyākak koṇṭiruntaṉar paḻantamiḻp pulavarkaḷ. aṉṉāriṉ perumikku iḵtōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum. ittakiya mēmpāṭuṭiya pulavar peru makkaḷāl tāṉ paḻantamiḻ nāṭu naṉṉeṟikkaṇ iṉitu naṭantu vantatu.
iyaṉmoḻi vāḻttu
pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ ceytiyōṭu, avaṉ muṉṉōr ceytikaḷiyum avaṉ mēlēṟṟik kūṟuvatu - iyaṉmoḻi vāḻttu eṉappaṭum. ceyti - vīram, koṭi, pukaḻ mutaliyaṉa. itaṉ payaṉ, ivaṉum attakiya ceyal ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatē yākum. iyal - iyalpu.
kaṭinili
oru talivaṉiṭam paricil nāṭic ceṉṟa pulavar, tamvaravit talivaṟkut terivikkumpaṭi vāyilōṉiṭam kūṟiviṭṭu, avaṉ talivaṉiṭam ceṉṟu kūṟivarum vari, vaḻinaṭantu vanta varuttan tīra veḷit tiṇṇiyil uṭkārntiruppar. itu kaṭinili eṉappaṭum. talivaṉ vantu pulavari aḻittuc celvāṉ. kaṭi  -vāyil. itu, pulavar perumakkaḷiṉ cevviyaṟintu naṭakkum tiṟap pāṭṭiyum, paḻantamiḻc celvarkaḷ pulavariṭattum, tamiḻ moḻi yiṭattum vittirunta aṉpiyum paṭampiṭittuk kāṭṭuvatākum.
kaṇpaṭi nili
aracar araciyal aluval pārttuk koṇṭu iravil neṭu nēram tūṅkātiruppiṉ, maruttuvar avarippaṭuttut tūṅkumpaṭi kūṟuvar. itu, kaṇpaṭi nili eṉappaṭum. kaṇpaṭi - kaṇpaṭutal - tūṅkutal. itaṉāl, paḻantamiḻaracarkaḷ tam meyvaruttam pārātu kuṭimakkaḷ nalattilēyē kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy iruntu vantamiyum, aracar uṭal nalattuṭaṉ iruntu nāṭṭi naṉku kākka vēṇṭum eṉpatē ivvāṟu kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiyāl, kuṭi viḷaṅkum. iṉṟu araṇmaṉi maruttuvar iruppatu pōlavē, aṉṟum iruntuvantami tamiḻarkku ēkkaḻuttam taruvatoṉṟākum. paḻantamiḻarkaḷ maruttuva nūṟ pulami mikkiruntamiyum peṟappaṭum.
vēḷvi nili
kaṟṟā pālcurantu koṭuttalip pōla aracar variyātu koṭuttalik kūṟutal - vēḷvinili eṉappaṭum. vēḷvi - koṭi.
“koṭiyaḷi ceṅkōl kuṭiyōmpal nāṉkum
 uṭiyaṉām vēntark koḷi.” (kuṟaḷ)
eṉa vaḷḷuvarum koṭiyi muṟkūṟutal kāṇka. koṭuttal - taḷarnta kuṭikaṭku vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭuttal.
viḷakku nili
vēlum, vēṟṟaliyum oṉṟupaṭṭu nērāka amintuḷḷami pōla, ceṅkōlum, viḷakkum oṉṟupaṭṭu nērāka amika veṉap pulavar vāḻttutal - viḷakkunili eṉappaṭum. atāvatu, koḻuntu viṭṭeriyum viḷakkup pōlac ceṅkōl ōṅkuka veṉa vāḻttutalām. vēltali - vēliṉ paṭam. vēl - uvami. viḷakku aciyāmal nērākak koḻuntuviṭṭerital pōla, ceṅkōlum vaḷiyāmal nērāka nilittirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu; muṟi tavaṟāmal āḷa vēṇṭu meṉpatu. itaṉāl, pulavar perumakkaḷ nāṭṭumakkaḷ nalattil koṇṭirunta ārvamum akkaṟiyum iṉitu viḷaṅkum.
vāyuṟi vāḻttu, ceviyaṟivuṟūu, puṟanili vāḻttu
immūṉṟum pulavar perumakkaṭkut talivar māṭṭuḷḷa aṉpiṉ aṟikuṟiyākum. muṉpu kittup piṉpu iṉpantarum vēmpum, kaṭuvum pōla, muṉpu kaṭumiyāyp piṉpu naṉmi payakkum veyya coṟkaḷik kūṟi uṇmiyi aṟivuṟuttuvatu - vāyuṟi vāḻttu eṉappaṭum. kaṭu-naccu maruntu. uṟi - maruntu. vēmpiṉālāya maruntum, naccu maruntum muṉpu kacantu piṉpu nōy tīrttu naṉmi ceytal pōla, naṉmi karutic colluṅ kaṭuñ collum attakiyavātal kāṇka.
cerukkuk koḷḷāmaṟ periyōrkaḷiṭam aṭaṅki naṭattal kaṭami eṉa aṟivuṟuttuvatu - ceviyaṟivuṟūu eṉappaṭum. ceviyil aṟivuṟuttal.
vaḻipaṭu teyvam niṉṉip pātukākka, kuṟiyāta celva muṭaṉ eṉṟum polivuṟṟu vāḻka veṉa vāḻttuvatu - puṟanili vāḻttu eṉappaṭum. ulakam pulavarkaṭ paṭṭatākaliṉ, ulakik kākkum araci vāḻtti ulaki vāḻvittal pulavar kaṭami yāyiṟṟu.
kikkiḷi
kikkiḷi - orutalik kāmam. itu, kāmañcālā iḷami yōḷ vayiṉ oruvaṉ koṇṭa kikkiḷiyum (akat - 50), iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉkaṇ talivaṉ māṭṭu nikaḻum kāṭci, aiyam, teḷital, tēṟal ākiya, ‘muṉṉiya nāṉkum muṉṉataṟku’ eṉṟa kikkiḷiyum (akat - 52), kaṭavuḷi makkaḷum, makkaḷik kaṭavuḷum virumpum kikkiḷiyum (puṟat - 28) pōlātu, oruvaṉum oruttiyum kaḷavoḻukka moḻuki maṇantu koṇṭu iṉitu illaṟam naṭatti iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntu varum pōtu, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyit tuṟanta viṭattu, avaḷi ēṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi pulavarkaḷ avaṉi vēṇṭutalām. itu, peṇmakkaḷiṉ urimip pātukāpput toṇṭākum. āṇmakkaḷiṉ ātikka urimiyē itaṟkuk kāraṇamākum.
kaṇṇaki eṉṉum taṉ maṉiviyit tuṟanta viyāvik kōpperum pēkaṉiṭam, avaḷi ēṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi kapilar, paraṇar, ariciṟkiḻār, peruṅkuṉṟūrk kiḻār eṉṉum pulavar perumakkaḷ vēṇṭiṉami (puṟam 143 - 7) itaṟku ilakkiyamākum. oru ciṟu tavaṟum naṭipeṟāmal tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki eṉṉum vaṇṭiyi iṉitu naṭattic cellum poṟuppup pulavar perumakkaḷiyaṉṟō cārntuḷḷatu? aṉṉār poṟuppu maliyiṉum māṇap peritāka vaṉṟō uḷatu! vāḻka aṉṉār potunalam!
3. pukaḻtal (puṟa - 36)
itil talivariṉ perumi palapaṭap pukaḻappaṭukiṟatu. tuyileṭi nili : pācaṟikkaṇ maṉavarutta miṉṟit tuyilum aracaric cūtar eṉpōr tuyileḻupputal - tuyileṭi nili eṉappaṭum. eṭuttal - eḻupputal. veṟṟic ciṟappāl maṉavarutta milli. aṉṉāriṉ vīratti, veṟṟic ciṟappiṉip pukaḻntu pāṭit tuyileḻuppuvar. cūtar - at toḻiluṭiyār.
veḷḷaṇi
aracaṉ tāṉ piṟanta nāḷil āṇṭutōṟum pōr ceytal, ciṟi ceytal, koli ceytal mutaliyaṉaviṉṟi, ceruvoḻital - ciṟi viṭutal, koli yoḻital iṟi tavirtal, vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭuttal mutaliyaṉa ceytu, veḷḷuṭiyaṇintu, aruḷuḷḷa muṭiyavaṉāy iruppāṉ. itu veḷḷaṇi eṉappaṭum. itu perumaṅkalam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. piṟantanāṭ koṇṭāṭṭam iṉṟum naṭantu varutal kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
maṇṇumaṅkalam
aracaṉ tāṉ muṭipuṉinta nāḷi āṇṭutōṟum koṇ ṭāṭuvāṉ. muṭipuṉintu nīrāṭuvatāl itu maṇṇu maṅkalam eṉappaṭṭatu. maṇṇutal - nīrāṭutal.
ulaki niḻal ceytu kāppatākak koṟṟak kuṭiyip pukaḻtal - kuṭimaṅkalam eṉappaṭum. pakiyip porutu veṉṟa veṟṟi vāḷip pukaḻtal - vāḷmaṅkalam eṉappaṭum. pakivar matiliyaḻittu maṅkala mallātaṉa ceytāṉ, maṅkalamāka nīrāṭutal - maṉṉeyil aḻitta maṇṇumaṅkalam eṉappaṭum.
iviyellām veṟṟit tirunāḷākak koṇṭāṭappaṭum. tamatu veṟṟiyik koṇṭāṭutal makkaḷiṉ iyalpu tāṉē!
pulavarceyal
pulavariṭattiluḷḷa aṉpāl, avarip piriya maṉamillātu talivarkaḷ paricu koṭukkak kālan tāḻttuvar. atu kaṇṭa pulavar, ‘nāṉ pōkavēṇṭum; iṉṉaparicil vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu kēṭpar. itu, paricil kaṭiiya nili eṉappaṭum. kaṭi iya - kēṭṭa, uṭaṉē talivar tām kālan tāḻtta taṉ karuttik kūṟip paricil koṭuttup piriyāviṭi koṭuppar. paṟampiṟ kōmāṉ pāriyum, paḻiyaṉūrk kāriyum oḷaviyārkkuk koṭuttup paṟittum, kaḷik koṭṭuk koṭuttum kālan tāḻttāmi itaṟku ilakkiyamākum.
pulavar tamakku vēṇṭiyati vāyilōṉiṭam colli yaṉuppi, avvāyiliṭattē paricil peṟutalum uṇṭu. itu kaṭikkūṭṭu nili eṉappaṭum. kaṭi - vāyil, kaṭikkūṭṭutal - vāyiliṉ kaṇ paricu peṟutal. vāyiliṉiṉṟum paricu peṟuvatāl, itu kaṭinili eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. itu talivariṭam nēril iṉṉa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kēṭka muṭiyāta nāṇattāl ivvāṟu colliya ṉuppip peṟṟu vantaṉar pōlum.
paricil peṟṟapiṉ talivaṉ tāṉē viṭi koṭuttalum uṇṭu; pulavar kēṭka viṭi koṭuttalum uṇṭu. iṉṉum palavakiyiṟ paricu peṟṟu vantamiyip puṟanāṉūṟṟil kāṇka. ipparicil vāḻkkiyē taṉṉērilāta tamiḻi iṉitu pōṟṟi vaḷarttuvanta teṉka.
āṟṟuppaṭi
iyal, ici, nāṭakam, eṉṉum muttamiḻil, iyaṟṟamiḻip pulavar perumakkaḷ vaḷarttu vantatu pōlavē, ici, nāṭakat tamiḻik kūttarum, pāṇarum, porunarum, viṟaliyarum, vaḷarttu vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ centamiḻp pākkaḷip pāṭiyum āṭiyum tamiḻt talivarkaḷiṭam paricu peṟṟu vāḻntu vantaṉar. aṅṅaṉamē paricu peṟṟu varum vaḻiyil paricil peṟātu varuntiya niliyil uḷḷa kūttar mutaliyavarkaḷik kāṇiṉ, tām peṟṟa paricili aṉṉārkkuk kūṟi, avarkaḷi attalivariṭam aṉuppuvar. itu, āṟṟuppaṭi eṉappaṭum. āṟu-vaḻi. āṟṟuppaṭuttal - vaḻip paṭuttal, avari attalivariṭam aṉupputal. aṅṅaṉam aṉuppum pōtu, tām peṟṟa paricilōṭu, talivaṉ perumi yiyum virittuk kūṟuvar. itu avaravar peyarāṉē kūttarāṟṟup paṭi, pāṇarāṟṟuppaṭi, porunarāṟṟuppaṭi, viṟaliyāṟṟuppaṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkum. pulavarāṟṟuppaṭiyuṅkoḷka. kūttari āṟṟuppaṭuttal - kūttarāṟṟuppaṭiyākum. eṉṉē pulavar perumakkaḷiṉ perun taṉmi! tam pōlavē piṟarum vāḻa vēṇṭum eṉṉum aṉṉāratu peruntaṉmiyē paḻantamiḻ makkaḷi iṉitiyakki vantatu. pattuppāṭṭiluḷḷa perum pāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, ciṟupāṇāṟṟup paṭi, porunarāṟṟup paṭi ivaṟṟiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum.
pulavaraṉpu
nāḷnimittam, puḷnimittam, piṟa nimittaṅkaḷāl pāṭāṇ ṭalivaṉukkut tīṅkuṇṭākumeṉa añci, attalivaṉukku avaṟṟāl yātoru tīṅkum nērāmal irukkavēṇṭumeṉap pulavarkaḷ vāḻttuvar. itu, ōmpaṭi eṉappaṭum. ōmpaṭi - pātukāppu, nimittam - kuṟi.
nāṭkuṟiyāvatu - oruvaṉ piṟanta nāḷoṭu kōḷporun tāmi. nāḷ - naṭcattiram. kōḷ - kirakam. vāṉa maṇṭalattil nāḷum kōḷum niṟkum nilikaṇṭu, ataṉāl annāḷuṭi yāṉukkut tītuṇṭākumeṉa añci avvāṟu tītuṇṭākkāmal irukka vēṇṭumeṉa vāḻttuvareṉka. itaṉāl, aṉṟu vāṉūlaṟivuṇmiyum, annampikkiyuṇmiyum peṟappaṭum.
puṭkuṟiyāvatu - putuppaṟavi kāṇutal, kūki mutaliya paṟavikaḷ kattutal mutaliyaṉa. piṟakuṟi - naṟcoṟku māṟākat tīccoṟ kēṭṭal, nari ūḷiyiṭutal mutaliyaṉa. talivarkaḷpāl pulavarkaḷ koṇṭirunta aṉpiṉ perukkē perukku! itu paṟṟittāṉ pōlum vaḷḷuvar, ‘aṉpukku muṇṭō aṭikkun tāḻ’ eṉṟatu!
nāḷnimittam, puḷnimittam, piṟa nimittaṅkaḷellām teyva nampikkiyiṉpāṟ paṭṭaṉavākum. teyvam eṉṉum talippil, ‘nimittam moḻipporuḷ teyvam’ eṉpatip pārkka.
9. eḻutiṇippotu (puṟa - 5)
ippakutiyil, veṭci mutaliya puṟattiṇi ēḻaṟkum potu vāṉa cila ceytikaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aḵtovvoṉṟum paḻantamiḻar vāḻviyaliṉ paṭappiṭippākum.
veṟiyāṭṭu
iḵtoru vakik kūttu; vēlaṉ veṟikoṇṭāṭutaliṉ ippeyar peṟṟatu. vēlik kiyilēntiyāṭutaliṉ - vēlaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avvēlaṉ kāntaṭpūvic cūṭiyāṭutaliṉ itu kāntaḷ eṉavumpaṭum. itu akattiṟkuc ciṟantatu; puṟattiṟkuc ciṟupāṉmi varum. eṉavē, itu akam, puṟam iraṇṭaṟkum potuvāyiṟṟu. itu akattiṇikkaṇ veṟiyāṭṭeṉavum, puṟattiṇik kaṇ veṟikkūtteṉavum peyar peṟum. puṟattil veṟi koṇṭāṭutal maṭṭumē. akattil vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭik kuṟicolvāṉ. vēlaṉ - oru kuṟiñci vakuppiṉaṉ. itaṉ viḷakkatti ‘veṟiyāṭṭu’ eṉṉum talippil kāṇka.
vaḷḷikkūttu
vaḷḷi eṉpatu - makaḷir āṭum kūttuḷ oruvaki. vaḷḷi - kuṟiñcinilap peṇ - kuṟatti; vēlaṉ makaḷir. vaḷḷik kūttu - kuṟatti pōlak kōlaṅkoṇṭāṭuvatu. āṇkaḷ avvāṟu kōlañceytāṭutalum uṇṭu. itu akattiṇikkaṇ vārātu. maṟakkuṭi makaḷir pōrkkaḷattil vaḷḷikkūttāṭi, maṟavarkku ūkka mūṭṭuvar. itaṉāl, paḻantamiḻp peṇkaḷiṉ maṟappaṇpum, nāṭṭaṉpum, kaṭappāṭṭuṇarcciyum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa vallavā? iṉṟum nāṭakaṅkaḷil kuṟavaṉ, kuṟattik kōlamiṭṭāṭuvatāl, vaḷḷikkūttiṉ paḻamiyum, iḷamiyum aṟintiṉpuṟaṟkuriyaṉavākum.
aṭiyāḷap pū
pōrkkaḷattē porumpōtu, iṉṉa vēntaṉ paṭi yāḷar eṉṟu terivataṟkāka, maṟavarkaḷ paṉampū, āttippū, vēppampū ākiya cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar eṉṉum muṭiyuṭi mūvēntar pūkkaḷil oṉṟic cūṭikkoḷvar. atāvatu, oru kaḷattil iruperuvēntar poruvaḻi, iruperum paṭiyum talimayaṅkip porumpōtu, yār veṉṟār eṉak kāṇpōr aṟitaṟkum, maṟavarkaḷ tammavari aṟitaṟkum aṉṉār tattam aracar pūvic cūṭik koḷvareṉka. tumpi mutaliya tiṇip pūkkaḷ irupaṭi yāḷarum cūṭutalāṉ iṉṉāreṉa eḷitil aṟiya muṭiyātu. ciṟṟaracar iruvar paki koṇṭu poriṉum aṉṉār maṟavarkaḷ tattam pēraracar pūkkaḷic cūṭik koḷvareṉka. itaṉāl, tamiḻakam muṭiyuṭi mūvēntar āṭcikkīḻ nilaviya kaṭṭup pāṭṭuṭaṉ eṉṟum iṭiyaṟātu iruntu vantateṉpatu peṟappaṭum. iḵtoṉṟē tamiḻar araciyaliṉ paḻamikkum perumikkum takka cāṉṟākum.
kaḻalnili
ōr iḷiñaṉ, perum paṭiyik kaṇṭu añci ōṭātu etir niṉṟu porutami kaṇṭa maṟavarkaḷ, avaṉ kāliṉkaṇ vīrakkaḻal puṉintu, avaṉic cūḻntāṭuvar. itu, kaḻalnili eṉappaṭum; kaḻalnilik kūttu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. maṟavarēyaṉṟi. maṟakkuṭi makaḷir āṭutalum uṇṭu. itaṉāl, āṇkaḷōṭu peṇkaḷum carinikar camāṉamāka vāḻntu vantami peṟappaṭum. attakiya nili iṉi eṉṟu ēṟpaṭuṅ kol!
uṉṉanili
piṉvāṅkāmal porum vēntaṉukku veṟṟiyuṇṭāka inta uṉṉamaram taḷirkkaveṉa maṟavarkaḷ paravuvatu uṉṉa nili eṉappaṭum. uṉṉam - oruvakik kāṭṭumaram. itu, ciṟanta nāṭṭaṉ piṉāl uṇṭākum oru vaki nampikkiyākum. viricci (puṟa -3) pōṉṟatē itu. iḵtum teyvap pakutiyēyākum. pūvinili - ‘teyvam’ eṉṉum talippiṟ kāṇka.
kuṟunila maṉṉar
cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar eṉṉum perunila maṉṉariṉ kīḻp pala kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷiruntu paḻantamiḻakatti iṉitu āṇṭu vantaṉar. kuṟunila maṉṉar perunila maṉṉari vellutalum ciṟupāṉmi uṇṭu. itu takācceyaleṉiṉum, oru cilar ceyta akkuṟṟatti eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār. itaṉiyē, ‘vēntalikkum kolkuṟumpu’ (kuṟaḷ - 735) eṉṟār vaḷḷuvar. kuṟumpu - ciṟṟaracar. kuṟunilamaṉṉar tam āṇmiyiṉāl perunila maṉṉari velvatāka vañciṉaṅ kūṟutalum itaṉpāṟpaṭum. veṭci maṟavar koṇṭa niriyik kuṟunila maṉṉar mīṭṭuttarutalum uṇṭu. ivar nirik kuṭiyōṉ ciṟṟaracarāvar. muṉṉatu vākiyiyum, piṉṉatu veṭciyiyum cērum. kuṟunila maṉṉarip perunila maṉṉarip pōlap pukaḻntu kūṟutalum uṇṭu. perunila maṉṉarukkuriya ellāc ciṟappum (marapu - 71) kuṟunila maṉṉarkkum uriyatākap pāṭutal. itu pāṭāṇic cārum. itaṉāl, paḻantamiḻp pēraracarkaḷ, ‘tāṉ’ eṉṉum cerukkiṉṟi otta maṉappāṉmi yuṭiyarāy iruntamiyum, pulavar perumakkaḷiṉ potu nōkkum peṟappaṭum.
piḷḷinili
taṉmēl varum koṭippaṭiyit tāṉoruvaṉē tāṅkutal; vāṭpōril pakivarik koṉṟu tāṉum iṟattal eṉṉum ivviraṇṭum piḷḷinili eṉappaṭum. piḷḷi - pōrkkaḷañ ceṉṟaṟiyāta vīra iḷiñaṉ. attakiya iḷiñar pōrkkaḷañ ceṉṟu ceyyum añcāmiyē piḷḷinili eṉappaṭum. tanti pōril iṟantataṉāl iḷamintaṉ pōrk kaḷañ ceṉṟu porutāṉeṉka. tamiḻar vīrattiṉ taṉic ciṟappiṟku iḵtōr eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. ittaki yavīra iḷañarip peṟṟu viḻuttaka voṭu viḷaṅkiṟṟup paṇṭit tamiḻakam. itu tumpiyic cārum.
piḷḷiyāṭṭu
vāṭpōril veṟṟi peṟṟa piḷḷiyik kaṇṭu nāṭṭu makkaḷ makiḻntu, muṭicūṭṭit taṅkaḷ aracaṉākkik koḷḷutal - piḷḷiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭum. ivaṉ vāṭpōril iṟavātu veṟṟi peṟṟa araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ. aracaṉ iṟantataṉāl, araciḷaṅkumaraṉi nāṭṭu makkaḷ aracaṉākkik koṇṭaṉar. itaṉāl, nāṭṭu makkaḷē aracaṉukaku muṭicūṭṭit taṅkaṭku aracaṉākkik koḷḷu talākiya potumakkaḷuṭaṉpāṭu peṟṟa muṭiyāṭciyē paḻan tamiḻakattil naṭantuvantami peṟappaṭum. oṉṟiyoṉ ṟaṭakki yāḷvatum. tam kaṭcik koḷkikkāka āṭci ceyvatumāṉa kaṭciyāṭciyi viṭa ittakiya muṭiyāṭciyē cālac ciṟantatākum.
naṭukal
pōril puṟaṅkoṭātu vīrattōṭu pōr purintu pukaḻi niṟuttip pōrkkaḷat toḻinta vīrarkku niṉivukkal nāṭṭi viḻāk koṇṭāṭivantaṉar nam muṉṉiyōr. akkallil maṟavaṉatu pērum, pukaḻum, uruvamum, poṟikkappaṭum. atu naṭukal eṉappaṭum. iṉṟuḷḷa kāṭṭuk kaṟuppaṇaṉ, muṉiyappaṉ mutaliya nāṭṭuppuṟat teyvaṅkaḷellām annaṭukaṟkaḷiṉ maṟupaṭippē yākum. paḻantamiḻ maṟavarkaḷ iṉṟupōl kūlippaṭiyā yirātu, nāṭṭuppaṟṟum, iṉappaṟṟum, poṟuppuṇarcciyum uṭiyarāy, nāṭṭikkāppatē tamatu nīṅkāk kaṭamiyeṉṉum uṟuti pūṇṭu, tantāyakamākiya tamiḻakatti uyiriṉum peritāka eṇṇi, vīrappōr purintu veṟṟiyuṭaṉ kāttuvantaṉar. nāṭṭukkāka uyirviṭṭa appaḻantamiḻ maṟavariṉ naṭukaṟkaḷ tamiḻ iḷiñarkaḷiṉ maṉakkaṇmuṉ kāṭciyaḷippatāka.
muṭivuri
tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāram kaṭal pōṉṟatu. atil vēṇṭuvatu, vēṇṭātatu eṉṉum poruḷē illi; ellām iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappuṭiyavi. tamiḻar vāḻkkik kaḷañciya mākiya poruḷatikāram tamiḻarkaḷāl tiṟantu pārkka muṭiyāmal mūliyiṟpōṭṭu mūṭivikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. tamiḻp potu makkaḷ paṭittaṟiyak kūṭiya niliyil atu vikkappaṭavilli. ivviḷakka nūl poruḷatikāram eṉpatu iṉṉaveṉpatit tamiḻ makkaṭku oruvāṟu aṟimukappaṭutti vikkumeṉpatu ematu nampikki.
annampikki uṇmiyākavē naṉṉampikkiyākum paṭiyāṉa naṟpayaṉpāṭoṉṟu kivarap peṟṟuḷḷatu. atu tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ naṟpayaṉākum. atāvatu,
tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattiṉ oṉpatu iyalkaḷil, mutal āṟu iyalkaḷiṉ karuttu viḷakkamē, ‘tolkāppiyar kālat tamiḻar’ eṉṉum ivviḷakka nūl ākum.
1. akattiṇiyiyal	4. kaṟpiyal
2. potuviyal	5. meyppāṭṭiyal
3. kaḷaviyal	6. puṟattiṇiyiyal
eṉṉum ivvāṟu iyalkaḷukkum viḷakkamāṉa virivuri eḻutap peṟṟu, ‘tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāram kuḻantiyuri’ eṉṉum annūl veḷivantu viṭṭatu. avvuriyip payiṉṟu, poruḷati kārattiṉ uṇmip poruḷiyuṇarntu, tam muṉṉōr vāḻkki muṟiyi aṟintu tamiḻ makkaḷ iṉpuṟu vārkaḷāka.



1. marapiyal -32
2. kumarikkaṇṭam - pak.36. kā. a.
1. oḷara - irāci. nāḷ - naṭcattiram. 2. nāḷ - acuviṉi, paraṇi mutaliya ikaravīṟṟu nāṭkaḷ. 3. tiṅkaḷ - vikāci, āṉi mutaliya ikaravīṟṟu mātaṅkaḷ
1. akattiyar ārāycci eṉṉum nūl pārkka.
2.'teyvam' eṉṉum talippip pārkka.
3. iyaṟṟamiḻāciriyar, maṉṉārkuṭi ma.nā. cōmacuntaram piḷḷi yavarkaḷ. puṟat -20 uri aṭikkuṟippu.
1. tirukkuṟaḷ kuḻantiyuri - mukavuri pārkka.
1. tirukkuṟaḷ kuḻantiyuri - mukavuri pārkka
3. tirukkuṟaḷ kuḻantiyuri pārkka.  

iṉṉūl
iṉṉūl
pulavar kuḻanti avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟu iṉṉūl. iṉṉūl (iṉnūl) eṉpatu iṉiya ilakkaṇam eṉpatē, nūl eṉṟē ōr ilakkaṇam iruntatu. ilakkaṇam kūṟa ēṟṟapā, akavaṟ cīrāl aminta nūṟpā eṉappaṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyap pērilakkaṇattiṉ piṉṉar nēminātam eṉṟoru nūl kuṇavīra paṇṭitarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. tolkāp piyatti nōkka atu ciṟiyatāka iruntami koṇṭu ciṉṉūl eṉavum vaḻaṅkiṉar. ataṉpiṉ vanta nūl pavaṇanti muṉivarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa naṉṉūl.
ciṉṉūl naṉṉūl eṉṉum peyarīṭukaḷ toṉṉūl eṉṟum peyarīṭṭit tōṟṟuvittatu. ataṉic ceytavar vīramā muṉivar. ciṉṉūr, naṉṉūl toṉṉūl eṉpavaṟṟiṉ piṉṉē vantatu iṉṉūl. itaṉpiṉṉē pāvalar pālacuntaram avarkaḷ teṉṉūl iyaṟṟiṉar.
oru nūl peyarīṭu, piṉṉavarkaḷ uḷḷattil patintu vaḷarum niliyi viḷakka ītoru cāṉṟām.
tolkāppiya ilakkaṇam eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṉum muppakuppuk koṇṭatu. poruḷ eṉṉum pakuppilēyē yāppum aṇiyum cērap peṟṟatu. poruḷ eṉpatilēyē akap poruḷ puṟap poruḷ eṉpaṉavum koṇṭatu.
naṉṉūlil eḻuttum collumē kiṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṟiyaṉār akap poruḷ nampiyakap poruḷ māṟaṉ akapporuḷ eṉap poruḷ nūl taṉiyē uruvāyiṉa. puṟap poruḷ kūṟap puṟap poruḷ veṇpāmāli uṇṭāyatu. yāppiyal kūṟa, yāpparuṅ kalam, yāpparuṅkalak kāriki eṉpaṉa uṇṭāyiṉa. aṇi ilakkaṇam kūṟat taṇṭiyalaṅ kāram uṇṭāyatu. māṟaṉ alaṅkāram eṉpatum tōṉṟiyatu.
aintilakkaṇaṅkaḷum kūṟum vakiyil ilakkaṇa viḷakkam mutalākac cila nūlkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. pulavar kuḻanti iyaṟṟiya iṉṉūl eḻuttu, col, yāppu, aṇi eṉpavaṟṟik kūṟuvatu āyiṟṟu. poruḷ ilakkaṇam itil illi. innūṟ poruḷkaḷum irunūṟṟuppattu nūṟpākkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa.
nūliṉ uṭpirivu iyal ākum, iṉṉūlil oṉpatu iyalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. peritum naṉṉūlit taḻuvi eḷimiyum iṉimiyum amiya iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nūl iḵtām.
nūṟ ceṟivu karutic cila nūṟpākkaḷ eḷimiyip puṟantaḷḷik kaṭumi koṇṭum perukkamiṉṟic curukkam kāṇac ceṟivu koṇṭum nūl ceyyappaṭṭami innūlil kāṇalām ātalāl, muṟṟāka eḷiyatu eṉak koḷḷaṟku illi.
tām virumpum amivukkut taka naṉṉūl nūṟpākkaḷic coṟkaḷ toṭarkaḷ māṟṟi amittuk koṇṭami pōṟṟak kūṭiyataṉṟu. muṉṉōr moḻi poruḷik kūṟal, muṉṉōr moḻiyi avvāṟē kūṟal eṉa irumarapum irukkavum iṭip pāṭu ceytal mūlanūlukku iṭarp pāṭum ākkuvatām. āciriyar ceyta peyarīṭu, innūṉ muṟi iṉmuṟi eṉpatēyām. āciriyar tam mūla mutaṟ patippuk kāṇa vāyttilatu. pulavar vaṭivēlaṉār urippatippiṉ mūlam koṇṭē uri eḻuta vāyttatu. oppiṭṭup pārkka ammūlam illāmiyāl mūlattil cila kuṟikaḷ tōṉṟi, uri kāṇum iṭaric cila nūṟpākkaḷ ākkukiṉṟaṉa.
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


eḻuttiyal
1. eḻuttum ataṉ vakikaḷum
moḻimutaṟ kāraṇa oliyē eḻuttām;
atu, mutal cārvu eṉa iruvakip paṭumē.
(uriviḷakkam) moḻikku mūlamāka iruppatu oliyām; av voliyālēyē eḻuttu ākum. avveḻuttu mutal eḻuttu eṉṟum cārvu eḻuttu eṉṟum iruvakippaṭum.
olikku muṟpaṭṭatu ōci eṉpatu. atu poruṭ kuṟipput tārātatu; iṭi, caṅka, alimuḻakku, iru mutal pōlvaṉa. ōci vēṟu oli vēṟu eṉpati,
"ōci oliyellām āṉāy nīyē"
eṉṉum apparaṭikaḷ vākkāl aṟiyalām
2. mutal eḻuttu
uyirum uṭampum ām muppatum mutalē.
(u-m) mutal eḻuttu eṉpavi, uyir eḻuttu eṉṉum paṉṉiraṇṭum, mey eḻuttu eṉṉum patiṉeṭṭum kūṭiya muppatu eḻuttukaḷumām.
uyireḻuttu	:	a, ā, i, ī, u, ū, e, ē, ai, o, ō,oḷa,
meyyeḻuttu	:	k,ṅ,c,ñ,ṭ,ṇ,t,n,p,m,y,r,l,v,ḻ, 					ḷ,ṟ,ṉ.
moḻikku aṭippaṭiyāy aminta eḻuttu mutal eḻuttu.
mutal eḻuttic cārntu varum eḻuttu cārpu eḻuttu.
3. uyir eḻuttiṉ iruvaki
a i u e ok kuṟil aintē:
ēṉi ēḻum neṭṭeḻuttu āmē.
(u-m) uyir eḻuttup paṉṉiraṇṭum tam oli cuviyāl (māttiriyāl) kuṟil eṉṟum neṭil eṉṟum iraṇṭākap pakukkappaṭum. 
kuṟil eḻuttu oru māttiri aḷaviṉatu. avi : a, i, u, e, o. kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉpatum itu.
neṭṭeḻuttu iraṇṭu māttiri aḷaviṉatu. avi: ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, ōḷa. neṭṭeḻuttu neṭil eṉavumpaṭum.
4. cuṭṭu eḻuttu
a i um mutal akattum puṟattum 
taṉiyē niṉṟu poruḷ cuṭ ṭummē.
(u-m) a, i, u eṉpaṉa cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷ. immūṉṟum collōṭu niṉṟum, collukku muṉṉē taṉittu niṉṟum kuṟittuk kāṭṭum eḻuttukkaḷām. collōṭu niṉṟu caṭṭuvatu akaccuṭṭu. e-ṭu. avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi; ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, ivi; uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvi eṉṉum ukara akac cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ muṟkālattup peruka vaḻaṅki, ikkālattu arukippōyiṉa.
am māṭu, ik kutiri, ukkari
a, i, u ākiya cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷ māṭu, kutiri, kari eṉpavaṟṟiṉ muṉ taṉiyāy (puṟattē) niṉṟu cuṭṭiṉa.
a - cēymi (tolivu) c cuṭṭu.
i - aṇmi (nerukka) c cuṭṭu.
u - cēymiyum ākāmal aṇmiyum ākāmal iṭiyē puṟam kāṭṭi niṟkum cuṭṭu. e-ṭu. uppakkam.
5. viṉā eḻuttu
e yā mutalil akattum 
puṟattum ā  ō īṟṟil puṟattum, ē mutal 
akattum īṟṟil puṟattum viṉāvē.
(u-m) e, yā, ā, ō,ē eṉpavi viṉā eḻuttukaḷ. cuṭṭeḻuttup pōlavē viṉā eḻuttum collōṭu niṉṟum (akattum) collukkup puṟattē niṉṟum (puṟattum) viṉāvāka amiyum.
e, yā eṉpaṉa colliṉ mutalil akattum puṟattum niṟkum.
e-ṭu	:	etu,evvīṭu,yāvaṉ, yāṅṅaṉam
ā, ō eṉpaṉa colliṉ iṟutiyil puṟattē varum.
e-ṭu	: 	naṇpaṉā, naṇpaṉō.
ē eṉpatu mutaṟkaṇ akattum, iṟutikkaṇ puṟattum varum.
e-ṭu	:	ētu, eṉṉē
6. mūviṉa meykaḷ
ka ca ṭa ta paṟa vali; ṅañaṇana maṉa meli;
yarala va ḻaḷa iṭi yām; mū viṉamē
(u-m)  k mutal ṉ muṭiya uḷḷa meyyeḻuttukaḷ patiṉeṭṭum mūṉṟu iṉamākap pirikkappaṭum. avi valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam eṉpaṉa.
valliṉam	:	k, c, ṭ, t, p, ṟ.
melliṉam	:	ṅ, ñ, ṇ, n, m, ṉ.
iṭiyiṉam	:	y, r, l, v, ḻ, ḷ
puḷḷi peṟum eḻuttu meyyeḻuttu. atu, oṟṟeḻuttu, uṭaleḻuttu eṉavumpaṭum.
7. iṉa eḻuttu
kuṟilukku neṭilum ai oḷakku i uvum
valikku meliyum iṉamā kummē.
(u-m) oṉṟaṉ iyalpi oṉṟi varuvatu iṉam ākum. avvakiyil kuṟṟeḻuttukaḷukku neṭṭeḻuttu iṉamām.
aā; iī; u,ū; e,ē; o,ō; ai eṉṉum eḻuttukkum oḷeṉṉum eḻuttukkum iṉamākiya
 kuṟṟeḻuttu illi. ātalāl avi tam oliyāl otta i eṉpatiyum u eṉpatiyum iṉamākak koṇṭu varum: ai ī : oḷa u.
valliṉa eḻuttukku melliṉam iṉamām.
kaṅa; caña; ṭaṇa; tana; pama; ṟaṉa.
(iṭiyiṉ eḻuttukaḷ āṟum ōriṉamām)
piṟappiṭam, muyaṟci aḷavu, poruḷ, vaṭivam mutaliyavaṟṟil cila oppumikaḷiyuṭiyatu iṉam eṉpār. naṉṉūlār (72).
8. cārpu eḻuttu
uyirmey, āytam uyiroṟṟu aḷapeṭi 
kuṟṟiyal ukaram kuṟṟiyal ikaram 
eṉum ivi āṟum cārpeḻuttu āmē.
(u-m) uyirmeyyeḻuttu, āyta eḻuttu, uyir aḷapeṭi, oṟṟaḷa peṭi,kuṟṟiyalukaram, kuṟṟiyalikaram eṉṉum āṟum cārpu eḻuttukaḷ. naṉṉūlār ivvāṟoṭum aikārak kuṟukkam, oḷakārak kuṟukkam, makarak kuṟukkam, āytak kuṟukkam eṉṉum nāṉkiyum kūṭṭic cārpeḻuttu  pattu eṉpār.
9. uyir meyyeḻuttu
uṭalmēl uyirūrntu oṉṟuvatu uyirmey
(u-m) meyyeḻuttiṉmēl uyir eḻuttu ēṟiya eḻuttu uyirmey eḻuttu eṉappaṭum. e-ṭu. k+a=ka. k+ā = kā.
meyyeḻuttu muṉṉum uyir eḻuttu piṉṉum niṟpatāl 'mey uyir' eṉal vēṇṭum eṉiṉ, uyir illāmal mey iyaṅkātu ātalāl 'uyirmey' eṉappaṭṭatu.
k mutaliya meykaḷ ikaram cērāmal olikkamāṭṭā! 'ik' eṉṟē collappaṭutal aṟika. ka eṉpatil k+a eṉa uyir piṉṉē varutalāl 'i' muṉṉē varavēṇṭiyatu illātu āyiṟṟu.
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭiṉ mēlum kūṭivaru vatāl uyir meyyeḻuttu irunūṟṟup patiṉāṟu ākum. ivi cārpu eḻuttukaḷ. mutal eḻuttukaḷ alla. mutal eḻuttukaḷ muppatē.
10. āyta eḻuttu
kuṟil val uyir mey iṭivarum āytam
(u-m) kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉatāka uyiroṭu kūṭiya valleḻuttu āṟaṉuḷ oṉṟaṉ mēlatāka iṭiyē āytam varum.
e-ṭu: 	eḵku,; kaḵcu; aḵtu; paḵṟi
āytattiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum kuṟṟeḻuttu iruttal aṟika. iraṇṭu ciṟakukaḷ aṭittu eḻuppa eḻumpum paṟaviyiṉ uṭal pōla āytam iyaṅkum eṉpar. colliṉ mutalākavō, neṭṭeḻutti aṭuttō āytam varāmi aṟika.
uyir aḷapeṭi
11. ici niṟi aḷapeṭi
icikeṭiṉ neṭil aḷapu eḻum; kuṟil kuṟiyē.
(u-m) ceyyuḷil ōci kuṟiyumiṭattu neṭṭeḻuttu taṉ aḷavil kūṭi olittu ōciyi nirappum. aḷapeṭukkum neṭṭeḻuttiṉ iṉamākiya kuṟilē ataṉ aṭiyāḷamāka niṟkum.
uyir eḻuttu taṉ aḷavil mikutalāl uyir aḷapeṭi āyatu. colliṉ mutal iṭi kaṭi ākiya : mūviṭaṅkaḷilum iv aḷapeṭi varum.
e-ṭu :	"ōotalvēṇṭum"
		"uṟāarkku uṟu nōy"
		"paṭā a paṟi"
12. iṉṉici aḷapeṭi
kuṟilneṭil āki aḷapu eḻal iṉṉici.
(u-m) iṉiya ōci uṇṭākkutal poruṭṭuk kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttu āki aḷapeṭuttāl ataṟku iṉṉici aḷapeṭi eṉpatu peyar.
e-ṭu:	koṭuppatūum keṭṭārkkuc cārpāymaṟ ṟāṅkē
	eṭuppa tūum ellām maḻi
ippāṭalil, keṭuppatum, eṭuppatum eṉa variṉum yāppuk kuṟṟam illi.  eṉiṉum, iṉiya ōci karutik kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttāki aḷapeṭiyum koṇṭami kāṇka.
13. collici aḷapeṭi
coltirintu aḷapueḻal col ici yāmē
(u-m) niṟkum col taṉ vaṭivam māṟi aḷapeṭiyum koṇṭāl collici aḷapeṭi eṉappaṭum.
peyarccol viṉieccac colliṉ iciyi niṟittaṟku aḷapeṭuppatu atu.
e-ṭu. uraṉnacii uḷḷam tuṇiyākac ceṉṟār
	    varaṉ nacii iṉṉum uḷēṉ.
uraṉ naci, varaṉ naci eṉṟu niṉṟālum ceyyuḷōci kuṟiyātu. viruppam eṉṉum poruḷ naci eṉṉum col, virumpi eṉṉum viṉi eccap poruḷ tarumāṟu aḷapeṭuttatāl collici aḷapeṭi āyiṟṟu. uyiraḷapeṭi ilakkaṇatti,
"icikeṭiṉ moḻimutal iṭi kaṭi nili neṭil
 aḷapu eḻum avaṟṟu avaṟṟu iṉakkuṟil kuṟiyē"
eṉṟē āciriyar tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉār (moḻimarapu 5) ataṉiyē muṉṉōr moḻiyāka naṉṉūlārum koṇṭār. (91) uriyāciriyarkaḷ aḷapeṭip pāṭalkaḷi āyntu pārttu im mūvakippaṭum eṉakkūṟiṉar. ataṉi nūṟpāvākap paṭittār pulavar kuḻanti.
14. oṟṟaḷa peṭi
ṅa ña ṇa namaṉa vayalaḷa āytam
aḷapu eḻum: avimikal ataṉ kuṟi yāmē.
(u-m) ceyyuḷil ōci kuṟiyumiṭattu ivaṇ kūṟappaṭṭa patiṉōr eḻuttukaḷum tam aḷavil mikum. av veḻuttē mīṇṭum ataṉ aṭiyāḷamāka varum.
iv voṟṟaḷapeṭi collil kuṟil iṇikkīḻum, kuṟiṟkīḻum varum. colliṉa iṭi, kaṭi ākiya īriṭaṅkaḷi lum varum.
e-ṭu:	"ilaṅṅku veṇpiṟi"-	kuṟil iṇikkīḻ iṭi.
	"eṅṅkuiṟivaṉ"	-	kuṟiṟ kīḻ iṭi.
	"maṭaṅṅ kalanta"	-	kuṟil iṇik kīḻk kaṭi
	"naṅṅ kaḷaṅkaṟuppām"	-	kuṟil kīḻk kaṭi.
	"vilaḵḵki vīṅkiruḷ"	-	āytam kuṟiliṇik kīḻ iṭi.
	"eḵḵ kilaṅkiya"	-	āytam kuṟiṟ kīḻk kaṭi.
15. kuṟṟiyal ukaram
neṭilōṭu āytam uyirvali meli iṭit
toṭar moḻi iṟutival ukaram kuṟukum.
(u-m) neṭil, āytam, uyir, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam ākiya toṭar moḻiyiṉ iṟutiyil niṟkum valliṉa ukaram taṉ aḷavākiya oru māttiriyil kuṟintu arimāt tiriyāyk kuṟṟiyal ukaram eṉṉum peyarip peṟum.
iṟutiyil niṟkum ukarattiṉ muṉniṟkum eḻuttik koṇṭē neṭil, āytam, uyir mutaliyavākap peyar peṟum. avvakiyāl.
	neṭil toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram	(nāku, kācu)
	āytat toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram	(eḵku, kaḵcu)
	uyirt toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram	(varaku, palācu)
	vaṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram	(kokku, kaccu)
	meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram	(kaṅku, pañcu)
	iṭit toṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram	(veytu, cālpu)
eṉap peyar peṟum.
16. muṟṟiyal ukaram
taṉikkuṟil aṭutta valliṉa ukaramum
melliṉa iṭiyiṉa ukaramum kuṟukā.
(u-m) taṉ ōci kuṟiyāta ukaram muṟṟiyal ukaramākum.
e-ṭu.	koṭu	(taṉikkuṟili aṭutta valliṉa ukaram)			kaṇu	(taṉikkuṟili aṭutta melliṉa ukaram)			kuru	(taṉikkuṟili aṭutta iṭiyiṉa ukaram)
ivi olikuṟiyāta ukaraṅkaḷ ātalāl muṟṟiyalukaramām.
"añcol maṭavārkku aruḷu"
"kaṇṇārak kāṇak katavu"
kuṟṟiyalukaramāka varavēṇṭiya veṇpāviṉ īṟu, iv vāṟu muṟṟiyalukaramāka aruki vantatiyum ēṟṟuk koṇṭa ṉar.
17. kuṟṟiyal ikaram
(u-m) yavvara  u  kkuṟaḷ tirinta ik kuṟukum.
kuṟṟiyalukaram niṉṟu varumoḻi mutalil yakara mutaṉ moḻi vantāl niṉṟa ukaram ikaramākat tiriyum. atu kuṟṟiya likaram eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu	:	nāku+yātu	=	nākiyātu
		eḵku+yātu	=	eḵkiyātu					varaku+yātu	=	varakiyātu					kokku+yātu=	kokkiyātu.
'miyā' eṉpatōr aciccol. atu kēḷ, cel eṉṉu miṭaṅkaḷil kēṇmiyā, ceṉmiyā eṉavarum. avviṭaṅkaḷil varum ikaramum kuṟṟiyalikaram ākum.
18. māttiri
mūṉṟu uyir aḷapu; iraṇ ṭām neṭil, oṉṟē
kuṟil, oṟṟaḷapeṭi; ari kuṟ ṟukaram
ikaram oṟṟōṭu āytam māttiri.
(u-m)
	uyiraḷapeṭikku māttiri mūṉṟu.
	neṭilukku māttiri iraṇṭu;					kuṟilukkum oṟṟaḷa peṭikkum māttiri oṉṟu;			kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalikaram oṟṟu āytam āyavaṟṟukku
	māttiriari.
mēlum makarak kuṟukkam āytak kuṟukkam eṉpavi kālmāt tiri koḷḷum eṉpatu aṟika.
koṇm ceṉm vāḻum vaki (makarak kuṟukkam)
kaltītu kaḵṟītu
muḷtītu muḵṭītu	(ivi āytak kuṟukkam)
19. māttiri viḷakkam
"iyalpeḻu māntar iminoṭi māttiri."
(naṉṉūl:100)
(u-m) iyalpāka māntar kaṇṇimikkum poḻutum, kinnoṭikkum poḻutum oru māttiriyākum.
	kaṇ imikkum poḻutu kaṇam.
	kin noṭikkum poḻutu noṭi.
	'kaṇap poḻutu,' 'noṭippoḻutu'- eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.			imippoḻutu, nimippoḻutu eṉpatum kaṇam.	nimiṭṭutal nimiṭam eṉpatum noṭippiṉ vaḻiyatē.
20. māttirik kūṟu
uṉṉal kālē; uṟuttal ariyē;
muṟukkal mukkāl; viṭuttal oṉṟē.
(u-m) ki noṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉittal kāl māttiri;naṭuviralip peruviralōṭu ūṉṟutal ari māttiri; viralkaḷ. iraṇṭiyum muṟukkuvatu mukkāl; muṟukkiya oli veḷippaṭṭu viṭṭāl oru māttiri.
oru māttiriyi ēṉ nāṟkūṟu ākkik kāṭṭavēṇṭum eṉṟāl kāl māttiri, arimāttiri oliyuṭiya eḻuttukaḷum iruppatāl eṉka.
21. eḻuttukaḷiṉ potup piṟappu
uḷ eḻum oli uram kaṇṭam ucci
mūkkuuṟṟu, itaḻnā pal aṇat toḻiliṉ
vevvēṟu eḻuttuoli yāyvaral piṟappē
(u-m) uḷḷē iruntu eḻum oliyāṉatu mārpu, kaḻuttu, tali, mūkku ākiya nāṉku iṭaṅkaḷiyum porunti, utaṭu, nākku, pal mēl vāy ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muyaṟcikaḷāl vev vēṟu eḻuttoli kaḷākat tōṉṟutal eḻuttukaḷiṉ piṟappākum.
uram - mārpu; kaṇṭam - kaḻuttu; aṇam - mēlvāy.
22. eḻuttukaḷiṉ iṭap piṟappu
valimārpu uṟum; iṭi yōṭu uyir miṭalu uṟum;
melimūkku iṭam peṟum; tali uṟum āytam.
(u-m)
	valleḻut toli, mārpi iṭamākak koṇṭupiṟakkum iṭiyeḻuttum uyireḻuttum,  kaḻutti iṭamākak  koṇṭupiṟakkum melleḻuttu, mūkki iṭamākaka koṇṭu piṟakkum āyta eḻuttu, taliyi iṭamākak koṇṭu piṟakkum
mārpu, kaḻuttu, mūkku, tali eṉpavi muṟiyē ēṟu variciyil amintaṉam aṟika.
23. moḻi mutal nili
paṉṉīr uyirum kacatapa ṅañanama
yava īr aintu uyir meyyum moḻimutal.
(u-m) paṉṉiraṇṭu uyir eḻuttukaḷum, ka, ca, ta, pa, ṅa, ña, na, ma, ya, va eṉṉum pattu uyir mey eḻuttukaḷum colliṉ mutalāka varum.
e-ṭu:aṉpu, ārvam, irakkam, īki, uṇmi, ūrti, eṇ, ēr, aintu, oṉṟu, ōṭam, ōḷavi.
kali, caṅku, taṅkam, paḻam, ṅaṉam, ñaṇṭu, naṉṟu, maṉam, yavaṉar, vaḷavaṉ.
24. moḻi iṟuti nili
āvi ñaṇa namaṉa yarala vaḻaḷa mey
kuṟṟiyal ukaram mū eṭṭum īṟē.
(u-m) taṉittum meyyōṭum varum paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirkaḷum, ña, ṇa, na, ma, ṉa, ya, ra, la, va, ḻa, ḷa ākiya patiṉoru meyyeḻuttu kaḷum, kuṟṟiyalukaram ākiya irupattu nāṉkum colliṉ iṟuti eḻuttukaḷām.
e-ṭu: ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō eṉavum,
viḷa, palā, kari, tī, kaṭu, pū, cēe, tē, ti, no, pō, ku eṉavum,
uriñ, maṇ, porun, maram, poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vāḷ aḵku eṉavum varum.
25. pōli
ōreḻuttu uḷ iṭattu ōr eḻuttu uṟṟup
poruḷvēṟu paṭātatu pōli ākum.
(u-m) ōr eḻuttu irukkum iṭattil vēṟōr eḻuttu vantu, poruḷ vēṟupāṭu illāmal atē poruḷ taruvatu pōli ākum. oṉṟip pōla varuvatu pōli.
moḻi mutal iṭi kaṭi ākiya mūviṭaṅkaḷilum pōli varum.muṟṟum varuvatum uṇṭu.
e-ṭu:	mayal-miyal		(mutal)
	kaliyam-kalayam		(iṭi)
	pantal-pantar		(kaṭi)
	aintu-añcu		(muṟṟum)
26. mutaṟpōliyum iṭippōliyum
akaram ca ña ya muṉ mutal iṭi aiyām.
(u-m) collukku mutalākavum iṭiyākavum varum ca, ña, ya eḻuttukaḷiṉ muṉ akaramum aikāramum vēṟupāṭu illāmal oppa naṭakkum.
e-ṭu:	pacal-pical
	mañcu-miñcu
	mayal-miyal.
27. iṭippōli
aikāṉ yavvaḻi naḵkāṉ ñavvē.
(u-m) colliṉ iṭiyē varum aikārattiṉ piṉṉum, yakara meyyiṉ piṉṉum iyalpāka varum nakara meyyiṉōṭu ñakaramey ottu naṭakkum.
e-ṭu: ainnūṟu-aiññūṟu;	ceynniṉṟa-ceyññiṉṟa
28. kaṭippōli
kuṟil iṇi makaram ṉakara mām; piṟa
mūviṭap pōli moḻikaḷum uḷavē.
(u-m) kuṟil iṇinta makara oṟṟuc col vantāl makara oṟṟu ṉakara oṟṟāka māṟum. mēlum colliṉ mūṉṟiṭaṅkaḷilum pōli varum.
e-ṭu: nilam-nilaṉ;	pulam-pulaṉ
	  nūṟpā 25-iṉ urikāṇka.
2. peyariyal
29. colliṉ ilakkaṇam
ōreḻut tāṉum paleḻut tāṉum
iyaṉṟu poruḷ kuṟittu iyalvatu collē.
(u-m) ōr eḻuttāvatu, pala eḻuttukaḷ cērntāvatu poruḷ kuṟittu varumāṉāl atu col eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	ā (pacu)	-	ōreḻuttu
	āṭu	-	īreḻuttu
	āṭal	-	mūveḻuttu
30. colliṉ taṉmi
orumoḻi toṭarmoḻi potumoḻi yāki
irutiṇi aimpāl īreṇ mū iṭattu
iyalpu takuti vaḻakkoṭu ceyyuḷiṉ
veḷippaṭi kuṟippiṉ virippatu collē.
(u-m) moḻiyāvatu col. oru moḻiyākavum, toṭar moḻiyākavum, potu moḻiyākavum col varum. uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi ākiya irutiṇikaḷiyum, āṇpāl peṇpāl palarpāl oṉṟaṉpāl palaviṉpāl ākiya aimpālkaḷi, orumi paṉmiyākiya iraṇṭu eṇkaḷiyum, taṉmi muṉṉili paṭarkkiyākiya mūviṭaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭu varum. iyalpu vaḻakku takuti vaḻakku eṉpavaṟṟi uṭiyatākavum irukkum. ceyyuḷil veḷippaṭiyum kuṟippum ākiya poruḷoṭum niṟkum.
ivaṟṟi muṟiyāka mēlē colkiṟār nūlāciriyar.
31. mūvaki moḻi
orumoḻi oruporuḷ; toṭarmoḻi palaporuḷ;
potumoḻi irumiyum ēṟkum eṉpa.
(u-m) 
	orumoḻi, oruporuḷit tarum.toṭarmoḻi, pala poruḷkaḷit tarum.	potumoḻi, taṉimoḻipōl oru poruḷiyum, toṭar moḻipōl pala poruḷkaḷiyum tarum.
e-ṭu:	tāmari-orumoḻi oruporuḷ.
	tāmari malar. toṭarmoḻi palaporuḷ.
	tāmari: tā+mari = tāvum māṉ.	potumoḻi.
	tām+ari= tammuṭiya ari.
32. irutiṇi
makkaḷ uyartiṇi; maṟṟavi aḵṟiṇi;
teyvap peyarum uyartiṇi eṉpa.
(u-m) makkaḷik kuṟippatu uyartiṇi. makkaḷ tavirnta maṟṟavaṟṟik kuṟippatu aḵṟiṇi; (al+tiṇi) uyarvu allāta tiṇi; teyvap peyariyum uyartiṇi eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
e-ṭu: avaṉ, avaḷ; maram kal; murukaṉ.
33. uyartiṇippāl
āṇ peṇ palar eṉum muppāl uyartiṇi
(u-m) āṇpāl,peṇpāl,palarpāl eṉṉum mūṉṟu pālkaḷum uyartiṇip pālkaḷām.
e-ṭu: nampi; naṅki; cāṉṟōr.
34. aḵṟiṇippāl
oṉṟē paleṉum irupāl aḵṟiṇi.
(u-m) oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa iruvakip pālkaḷi yuṭiyatu aḵṟiṇi. pāl = pakuppu.
e-ṭu:	māṭu; māṭukaḷ
	mali; malikaḷ
35.eṇ
oṉṟē orumi, oṉṟu ala paṉmi.
(u-m) oṉṟik kuṟippatu orumi; oṉṟaṟku  mēṟpaṭṭavaṟṟik kuṟippatu paṉmi. eṇṇikki paṟṟiyatu ātalāl eṇ eṉappaṭṭatu.
36. iṭam
pēcuvōṉ taṉmi; kēṭpōṉ muṉṉili;
pēcap paṭuvōṉ paṭarkki iṭamē.
(u-m) pēcupavaṉ iṭam taṉmi; kēṭpavaṉ iṭam muṉṉili; pēcappaṭupavaṉ iṭam paṭarkki.
	e-ṭu:	yāṉ, nāṉ	-	taṉmi
		nī, nīvir	-	muṉṉili
		avaṉ, avar	-	paṭarkki.
37. vaḻakku
ilakkaṇam uṭiyatu, ilakkaṇap pōli,
marūu eṉ ṟākum mūvaki iyalpum;
iṭakkar aṭakkal, maṅkalam, kuḻūukkuṟi
eṉummut takutiyoṭu āṟām vaḻakkiyal.
(naṉṉūl:267)
(u-m) vaḻakkukaḷ iruvakippaṭum. avi iyalpu vaḻakku, takutivaḻakku eṉpaṉa. ivvaḻakkukaḷ ovvoṉ ṟum mūvakippaṭum.
ilakkaṇam uṭiyatu
ilakkaṇappōli	iyalpu vaḻakku.
marūu
iṭakkar aṭakkal
maṅkalam	takuti vaḻakku.
kuḻūukkuṟi
vāḻkkiyil iyalpāka amintatu iyalpu vaḻakku. itu takkatu eṉa aṟivarkaḷāl amittuk koḷḷap paṭṭatu takuti vaḻakku.
e-ṭu:
ilakkaṇam uṭiyatu	:	maṇ,viṇ
ilakkaṇap pōli	:	ilmuṉ-muṉṟil							ilvāy-vāyil
marūu		:	uṟanti-uṟiyūr						putuvi-putuccēri
			putuki-putukkōṭṭi.
iṭakkar aṭakkal	:	oṉṟukku, iraṇṭukku, kāl					kaḻuvutal.
maṅkalam	:	viḷakki vaḷaravi.						ōli-tirumukam.
kuḻūukkuṟi	:	veḷḷik kutiri-kaḷ						paṟi-poṉ.
	ilakkaṇak col muṉpiṉ māṟiniṟpatu ilakkaṇap pōli.			col curuṅki māṟi varuvatu marūu. maruvu - taḻuvu. takkōraviyil kūṟak kūṭāta col iṭakkaraṭakku.
	maṅkalam allati maṅkalamākak kūṟuvatu maṅkalam. oru
	kuḻuviṉar taṅkaḷukkuḷ vaḻaṅkum kuṟippuc col kuḻūukkuṟi.
38. ceyyuḷ
palvakip poruḷiṉ uyirkkuuṭal pōlpala
collāl poruṭku iṭam āka aṟiviṉil
vallōr aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉa ceyyuḷ
(naṉ.268:taḻuval)
(u-m) aṟivumikkōrāl cemmiyākavum aḻakākavum ceyyappaṭukiṟatu ceyyuḷ ākum. atu palvakip poruḷ kaḷukku iṭamāka irukkum. atu, uṭali iyakkum uyirip pōl collum poruḷum amintu iyakkamuṭiya tāy viḷaṅkum.
"tāmari puriyum kāmar cēvaṭip
 pavaḻat taṉṉa mēṉit tikaḻoḷik 
 kuṉṟi yēykkum uṭukkik kuṉṟiṉ
 neñcupaka eṟinta añcuṭar neṭuvēl
 cēvalaṅ koṭiyōṉ kāppa
 ēma vikal eytiṉṟāl ulakē"
ik kuṟuntokik kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuc ceyyuḷ, "iḵtu ellāk kuṟṟamum tīrnta ceyyuḷ eṉṟu pārāṭṭappaṭu kiṉṟatu, yāpparuṅ kalakkāriki uri "                       (oḻiyiyal.9).
39. veḷippaṭi kuṟippu
oṉṟuoḻi potuccol vikāram takuti
āku peyaroṭu aṉmoḻi eṉṟā,
mutaṟkuṟip pōṭu tokikkuṟip peṉṟā,
kuṟippu viṉiyōṭu iṉṉa piṟavum
kuṟippil tarumoḻi; avvaṉa veḷippaṭi.
 (naṉṉūl 269 taḻuval)
(u-m) uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇiyākiya irutiṇi āṇpāl peṇpāl eṉṉum pālkaḷuḷ oru pāli vilakkic collum collum, valittal melittal mutaliya vikārac collum, mūvakit takuti vaḻakkuc collum, mutaṟkuṟippuc collum, tokik kuṟippuc collum, kuṟippu viṉic collum, ittakiya piṟavum kuṟippāl irutiṇi aimpāl poruḷkaḷit tarum coṟkaḷākum, maṟṟavi veḷippaṭiyāy apporuḷ tarum coṟkaḷākum.
makavu īṉṟār aiyar - āṇkaḷi vilakkiya peṇkaḷ
āyiravar kaḷam ceṉṟār - makaḷiri vilakkiya āṭavar. 
oṉṟoḻipotuccol
mari malar - tāmari malar (vikāram)
kālkaḻuvi varal - maṅkala vaḻakku (takuti)
puḷi tiṉṟāṉ - ākupeyar.
poṟṟoṭi vantāḷ - aṉmoḻi
"ulakelām" - colluka - mutaṟkuṟippu
 mūvar - tokik kuṟippuc col.
 avar periyar - kuṟippu viṉiccol.
uṭal, uyir, aṉpu iṉṉavi veḷippaṭic coṟkaḷ.
40. ceyyuṭ col
peyarviṉi aṭutta iṭi uri yākiya
iyaṟcol tiricol eṉum tamiḻc colloṭu
ticivaṭa coṟkaḷum ceyyuḷil varumē
(u-m) ceyyuḷil peyar viṉi iṭi uriyākiya tamiḻ iyaṟcoṟkaḷum tiricoṟkaḷum ticic coṟkaḷum vaṭa coṟkaḷum varum, ic coṟkaḷi mēlē viḷakkic colkiṟār.
41. iyaṟcol, tiricol
evarkkum viḷaṅkum iyalpiṉatu iyaṟcol;
ceyyuḷ collām cemmoḻi tiricol.
(u-m) evarkkum eḷitil viḷaṅkum col iyaṟcol. aritil poruḷ viḷaṅkuvatāyc ceyyuḷil varuvatāy amintatu tiri col. (tirital -veḷippaṭa viḷaṅkāmi)
e-ṭu:	yāṉi, puli (iyaṟcol) 
		vēḻam, varimā (tiricol)
42. ticic col
vaṭacol allā ayaṟcol ticiccol
(u-m) vaṭacol allāta maṟṟi ayaṟ coṟkaḷel lām ticic col. palvēṟu ticikaḷil iruntu vantu vaḻaṅkum col.  centamiḻc collum vaṭamoḻic col lum allāmal vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ ellāmum ticiccol eṉak koṇṭār āciriyar. tamiḻil kalanta vēṟṟuc col vaṭacol ātalāl, āciriyar ac colli avvāṟē collutali vilakki, aritil accollic colla nēriṉ tamiḻ marapu keṭāmal accollic colluka eṉṟu āṇiyiṭṭār eṉpatu eṇṇat takkatu.
43 vaṭacol
potu eḻut tālum ciṟappueḻut tālum
īreḻut tālum iyintuṟum vaṭacol.
(u-m) tamiḻiṟkum vaṭamoḻikkum uriya potu eḻuttu kaḷālum, vaṭamoḻikku aminta ciṟappu eḻuttukaḷālum, potu, ciṟappu ākiya iruvaki eḻuttukaḷālum amintavi vaṭacoṟkaḷām. 
peyar
44. peyariṉ vakiyum ilakkaṇamum
iṭukuṟi mutalāp poruḷ mutal āṟoṭu
viṉikulam kiḷi aḷavu iṭappotup peyariṉ
vēṟṟumikku iṭaṉāyt tiṇipāl iṭattoṉṟu
ēṟpavum potuvum āvaṉa peyarē.
(u-m) iṭukuṟippeyar, kāraṇap peyar, kāraṇa iṭu kuṟippeyar, poruṭpeyar, iṭappeyar, kālappeyar, ciṉippeyar, paṇpup peyar, toḻiṟpeyar, viṉiyāl aṇiyum peyar, kiḷip peyar, eṇṇup peyar, eṭuttal peyar, mukattal peyar nīṭṭal peyar ūr mutalām iṭap peyar potup peyar ākiyavi eṭṭu vēṟṟumiyum cār taṟku iṭamākit tiṇi pāl iṭam ākiyavaṟṟil oṉṟaṉi ēṟpaṉavum palavaṟṟi ēṟpaṉavum āki varuvaṉa peyarkaḷ ākum.
ivaṟṟuḷ viṉiyāl aṇiyum peyar oṉṟum kālam kāṭṭum! kālam maṟṟavikāṭṭā!
ivaṟṟiṉ viḷakkam mēl varumāṟu:
45. iṭukuṟi mutaliya peyarkaḷ
iṭukuṟi kāraṇam kāraṇa iṭukuṟip
peyar potuc ciṟappiṉa īṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
(u-m) iṭukuṟippeyar, kāraṇappeyar, kāraṇa iṭukuṟip peyar eṉpaṉa potup peyar, ciṟappup peyar eṉa varum iṟuti oṉṟu allāta iṭattu, varuvaṉa.
e-ṭu:	maram	-	iṭukuṟip potuppeyar
	paṉi	-	iṭukuṟic ciṟappup peyar
	aṇi	-	kāraṇap potuppeyar
	muṭi	-	kāraṇac ciṟappup peyar
( naṉ.62. caṅkara).
oru kāraṇa iṭukuṟippeyar palavaṟṟukkup poruntum āyiṉum oṉṟaṟkē iṭukuṟippeyarpōla varuvatu.
e-ṭu:
	mukkaṇṇaṉ	-	mukkaṇṇiyuṭiya iṟivar palaruḷ
			civaṉukkē peyarāvatu.
	muḷḷi	-	muṭceṭikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ ōriṉac ceṭikkē
			peyarāvatu.
(naṉ. 62 irāmā).
46. poruḷ mutaliya peyarkaḷ
poruḷ iṭam kālam ciṉi kuṇam toḻiliṉ
aṭiyāp piṟakkum peyarum aḵtē.
(u-m) poruṭ peyar, iṭap peyar, kālap peyar, ciṉi (uṟuppu)p peyar kuṇa (paṇpu)p peyar, toḻiṟ peyar eṉap peyar āṟām. ivaṟṟiṉ aṭiyākap piṟakkum peyarkaḷum āṟām.
e-ṭu:
	poruṭpeyar-vēl;	poruḷaṭip peyar-vēlaṉ.
